{

    "SLOPTRIM ACTIVE - level: full\n\n# Sloptrim\n\nYou write prose like a careful human writer. This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. When the file guard flags a span, fix it and say nothing. The clean prose is the only output; the process is never narrated.\n\nAfter writing a prose file (.md/.txt), run: python \"/Users/masterman/.claude/plugins/cache/sloptrim/sloptrim/0.9.0/scripts/detect.py\" \"<file>\" and read _metrics.ai_tell_score. If the band is worse than the target - clean or light tells (score <= 40) - fix only the flagged spans, at most two passes, keeping rhythm variation (a flattened husk is as obvious as slop). For a deep rewrite, invoke the sloptrim skill."

68	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
69	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
70	    .get();
71	  return record?.status ?? null;
72	}
73	
74	function timestampMs(value: Date | string | number | null | undefined) {
75	  if (value instanceof Date) {
76	    return value.getTime();
77	  }
78	  const parsed = new Date(value ?? 0).getTime();
79	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
80	}
81	
82	async function loadRun(runId: string): Promise<RunRecord> {
83	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
84	  if (!run) {
85	    throw refusal(404, "Conversation not found");
86	  }
87	  return run;
88	}
89	
90	async function loadQueuedRecord(runId: string, messageId: string): Promise<QueuedRecord> {
91	  const record = await db
92	    .select()
93	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
94	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
95	    .get();
96	  if (!record || record.runId !== runId) {
97	    throw refusal(404, "Queued message not found");
98	  }
99	  if (record.status === "delivered") {
100	    throw refusal(409, "Queued message was already delivered");
101	  }
102	  if (record.status === "cancelled") {
103	    throw refusal(409, "Queued message was cancelled");
104	  }
105	  return record;
106	}
107	
108	async function selectOldestPendingRecord(runId: string): Promise<QueuedRecord> {
109	  const records = await db
110	    .select()
111	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
112	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
113	    .orderBy(asc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), asc(queuedConversationMessages.id));
114	  const pending = records.find((record) => record.status === "pending");
115	  if (!pending) {
116	    emitNamedEvent({
117	      kind: "queue.interrupt_refused",
118	      runId,
119	      workerId: null,
120	      queuedMessageId: null,
121	      reason: "no_user_intent",
122	      source: "escape",
123	    });
124	    throw refusal(409, "No queued message to interrupt");
125	  }
126	  return pending;
127	}
128	
129	async function resolveInterruptWorker(run: RunRecord, record: QueuedRecord): Promise<WorkerRecord | null> {
130	  const targetWorker = record.targetWorkerId
131	    ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, record.targetWorkerId)).get()
132	    : null;
133	  if (targetWorker && targetWorker.runId === run.id && !isCancelledWorkerStatus(targetWorker.status)) {
134	    return targetWorker;
135	  }
136	  const latest = await getLatestRunWorker(run.id, record.targetWorkerId);
137	  if (latest && latest.runId === run.id && !isCancelledWorkerStatus(latest.status)) {
138	    return latest;
139	  }
140	  return null;
141	}
142	
143	/**
144	 * Core control-plane flow shared by every interrupt entry point:
145	 * cancel the active turn, advance the worker turn fence into a delivery-safe
146	 * state, then deliver exactly one queued user message through the unified
147	 * worker stream. Emits named + execution events for every decision.
148	 */
149	async function interruptAndDeliver(args: {
150	  run: RunRecord;
151	  record: QueuedRecord;
152	  source: InterruptSource;
153	}): Promise<InterruptResult> {
154	  const { run, record, source } = args;
155	  const runId = run.id;
156	  const requestedAt = Date.now();
157	
158	  emitNamedEvent({
159	    kind: "queue.interrupt_requested",
160	    runId,
161	    workerId: record.targetWorkerId ?? null,
162	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
163	    source,
164	  });
165	  await recordExecutionEvent({
166	    runId,
167	    workerId: record.targetWorkerId ?? null,
168	    eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_requested",
169	    details: {
170	      summary: "User requested an interrupt-and-send for a queued message.",
171	      queuedMessageId: record.id,
172	      source,
173	    },
174	  });
175	
176	  const worker = await resolveInterruptWorker(run, record);
177	  if (!worker) {
178	    emitNamedEvent({
179	      kind: "queue.interrupt_refused",
180	      runId,
181	      workerId: record.targetWorkerId ?? null,
182	      queuedMessageId: record.id,
183	      reason: "worker_missing",
184	      source,
185	    });
186	    emitNamedEvent({
187	      kind: "error.surfaced",
188	      code: "queue.interrupt.refused",
189	      message: "No active worker is available to interrupt.",
190	      surface: "toast",
191	      runId,
192	    });
193	    throw refusal(409, "No active worker is available to interrupt");
194	  }
195	
196	  // Steer is cancel-and-replace, and it must be instant. Two rules make it so:
197	  //
198	  //   1. The local abort is authoritative. Tripping the running turn's signal
199	  //      tears down its in-flight request here and now; it needs no cooperation
200	  //      from an agent that may be wedged.
201	  //   2. `session/cancel` is best-effort. It tells the agent to stop the work it
202	  //      is doing, which is worth sending, but awaiting it put a possibly
203	  //      unbounded round trip on the user's critical path — a wedged adapter
204	  //      used to block the steer button indefinitely, and a failed cancel
205	  //      refused the steer outright with a 502.
206	  const cancelStartedAt = Date.now();
207	  const abortedLiveTurn = abortWorkerTurn(worker.id, "user steer");
208	  void Promise.resolve(cancelAgentTurn(worker.id)).catch((error) => {
209	    void recordExecutionEvent({
210	      runId,
211	      workerId: worker.id,
212	      eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_cancel_best_effort_failed",
213	      details: {
214	        summary: `Agent-side cancel for ${worker.id} failed after the turn was already aborted locally.`,
215	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
216	        error: errorMessage(error),
217	        source,
218	      },
219	    }).catch(() => undefined);
220	  });
221	  const cancelDurationMs = Date.now() - cancelStartedAt;
222	
223	  // Advance the fence and reset persisted worker state into a delivery-safe
224	  // shape in one mutation so immediate delivery cannot observe stale `working`.
225	  const cancelledAt = new Date();
226	  const generation = await advanceWorkerTurnGeneration(worker.id, {
227	    status: "idle",
228	    clearCurrentText: true,
229	    updatedAt: cancelledAt,
230	  });
231	
232	  emitNamedEvent({
233	    kind: "queue.interrupt_cancelled_turn",
234	    runId,
235	    workerId: worker.id,
236	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
237	    cancelDurationMs,
238	    source,
239	  });
240	  await recordExecutionEvent({
241	    runId,
242	    workerId: worker.id,
243	    eventType: "queued_message_turn_cancelled",
244	    details: {
245	      summary: `Interrupted the active turn for ${worker.id}.`,
246	      queuedMessageId: record.id,
247	      cancelDurationMs,
248	      abortedLiveTurn,
249	      source,
250	    },
251	  });
252	
253	  // Mark the row delivering and move the run back into an active state.
254	  const startedAt = new Date();
255	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
256	    action: "steer",
257	    targetWorkerId: worker.id,
258	    status: "delivering",
259	    lastError: null,
260	    updatedAt: startedAt,
261	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
262	  await db.update(runs).set({
263	    status: run.mode === "planning" ? "working" : "running",
264	    failedAt: null,
265	    lastError: null,
266	    updatedAt: startedAt,
267	  }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
268	
269	  emitNamedEvent({
270	    kind: "queue.interrupt_delivery_started",
271	    runId,
272	    workerId: worker.id,
273	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
274	    totalInterruptLatencyMs: Date.now() - requestedAt,
275	    source,
276	  });
277	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
278	
279	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(
280	    record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : [],
281	  );
282	  const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
283	    resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
284	    imagesInlined: true,
285	  });
286	
287	  const userMessage = {
288	    id: record.id,
289	    runId,
290	    role: "user" as const,
291	    kind: "checkpoint" as const,
292	    content: record.content,
293	    attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
294	    createdAt: startedAt,
295	  };
296	
297	  trackConversationBackgroundTask(
298	    deliverInterruptedQueuedMessage({
299	      run,
300	      worker,
301	      record,
302	      userMessage,
303	      workerContent,
304	      attachments: normalizedAttachments,
305	      generation,
306	      source,
307	      requestedAt,
308	    }).catch((error) => {
309	      console.error("Queued message interrupt delivery failed:", error);
310	    }),
311	    { runId },
312	  );
313	
314	  return {
315	    ok: true,
316	    message: serializeMessageRecord(userMessage),
317	    queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
318	      ...record,
319	      action: "steer",
320	      targetWorkerId: worker.id,
321	      status: "delivering",
322	      lastError: null,
323	      updatedAt: startedAt,
324	      deliveredAt: null,
325	    }),
326	    interruption: {
327	      status: "delivering",
328	      workerId: worker.id,
329	      cancelDurationMs,
330	    },
331	  };
332	}
333	
334	async function deliverInterruptedQueuedMessage(args: {
335	  run: RunRecord;
336	  worker: WorkerRecord;
337	  record: QueuedRecord;
338	  userMessage: {
339	    id: string;
340	    runId: string;
341	    role: "user";
342	    kind: "checkpoint";
343	    content: string;
344	    attachmentsJson: string | null;
345	    createdAt: Date;
346	  };
347	  workerContent: string;
348	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
349	  generation: number;
350	  source: InterruptSource;
351	  requestedAt: number;
352	}) {
353	  const { run, worker, record, userMessage, workerContent, attachments, generation, source, requestedAt } = args;
354	  const runId = run.id;
355	
356	  /**
357	   * A stale terminal write must never overwrite a newer interrupt delivery or a
358	   * row the user cancelled mid-flight, so every terminal mutation re-checks the
359	   * row status and the turn fence.
360	   *
361	   * Bailing out used to just `return`, which left the row in `delivering`
362	   * forever — a state nothing else ever resolves. So a superseded delivery is
363	   * released here instead:
364	   *
365	   * - `appended: false` — nothing was written yet, so the row goes back to
366	   *   `pending`: the message is still queued, visible, and retryable.
367	   * - `appended: true` — the message is already in the transcript and its id is
368	   *   the primary key of the `messages` row, so re-delivering it would collide.
369	   *   The row is closed as delivered and the supersede is recorded.
370	   */
371	  const releaseSupersededRow = async (appended: boolean) => {
372	    if (await queuedRowStatus(record.id) !== "delivering") {
373	      return;
374	    }
375	    const now = new Date();
376	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
377	      status: appended ? "delivered" : "pending",
378	      lastError: null,
379	      updatedAt: now,
380	      ...(appended ? { deliveredAt: now } : {}),
381	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
382	    await recordExecutionEvent({
383	      runId,
384	      workerId: worker.id,
385	      eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_superseded",
386	      details: {
387	        summary: appended
388	          ? `A newer turn took over after ${worker.id} already received this message.`
389	          : `Requeued the message for ${worker.id} because a newer turn took over before it was sent.`,
390	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
391	        appendedToTranscript: appended,
392	        source,
393	      },
394	    });
395	  };
396	
397	  const isStillCurrent = async (appended = false) => {
398	    if (await queuedRowStatus(record.id) !== "delivering") {
399	      return false;
400	    }
401	    if (await isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(worker.id, generation)) {
402	      return true;
403	    }
404	    await releaseSupersededRow(appended);
405	    return false;
406	  };
407	
408	  let appendedToTranscript = false;
409	
410	  try {
411	    await runWorkerTurn(worker.id, async () => {
412	      if (!(await isStillCurrent())) {
413	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
414	        return;
415	      }
416	
417	      await db.update(workers).set({
418	        status: "working",
419	        updatedAt: new Date(),
420	      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
421	
422	      const deliveredAt = new Date();
423	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
424	        id: userMessage.id,
425	        runId,
426	        workerId: worker.id,
427	        text: record.content,
428	        deliveredAt,
429	        attachments: attachments.map((attachment) => ({
430	          id: attachment.id,
431	          filename: attachment.name,
432	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
433	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
434	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
435	        })),
436	      });
437	      await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
438	      appendedToTranscript = true;
439	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
440	
441	      if (!(await isStillCurrent(true))) {
442	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
443	        return;
444	      }
445	
446	      const workerPrompt = run.mode === "direct" || run.mode === "commit"
447	        ? buildDirectWorkerPrompt(workerContent)
448	        : workerContent;
449	      const imageAttachments = resolveImageAttachments(attachments, getAppDataPath);
450	      let response;
451	      try {
452	        response = imageAttachments.length
453	          ? await askAgent(worker.id, workerPrompt, imageAttachments)
454	          : await askAgent(worker.id, workerPrompt);
455	      } catch (error) {
456	        if (!isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(errorMessage(error))) {
457	          throw error;
458	        }
459	
460	        const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
461	        const recreated = await recreateWorkerFromTranscript({
462	          run,
463	          worker: currentWorker ?? worker,
464	          nextUserPrompt: workerPrompt,
465	          source: "steer",
466	          reason: "provider_session_diagnostic_after_steer",
467	        });
468	        await db.update(workers).set({
469	          status: "working",
470	          updatedAt: new Date(),
471	        }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
472	        response = imageAttachments.length
473	          ? await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt, imageAttachments)
474	          : await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt);
475	      }
476	      const finishedAt = new Date();
477	
478	      // Before any terminal persistence, confirm a newer interrupt has not
479	      // superseded this delivery and the row was not cancelled.
480	      if (!(await isStillCurrent(true))) {
481	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
482	        return;
483	      }
484	
485	      await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
486	        run: { id: runId, mode: run.mode },
487	        workerId: worker.id,
488	        response,
489	        deliveredAt: finishedAt,
490	        userInputEntryId: userMessage.id,
491	      });
492	
493	      if (!(await isStillCurrent(true))) {
494	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
495	        return;
496	      }
497	
498	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
499	        status: "delivered",
500	        lastError: null,
501	        updatedAt: finishedAt,
502	        deliveredAt: finishedAt,
503	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
504	      await recordExecutionEvent({
505	        runId,
506	        workerId: worker.id,
507	        eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_delivered",
508	        details: {
509	          summary: `Delivered interrupted queued message to ${worker.id}.`,
510	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
511	          source,
512	        },
513	      });
514	      emitNamedEvent({
515	        kind: "queue.interrupt_delivery_finished",
516	        runId,
517	        workerId: worker.id,
518	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
519	        totalInterruptLatencyMs: Date.now() - requestedAt,
520	        source,
521	      });
522	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
523	    });
524	  } catch (error) {
525	    // A superseded turn lost a race; an aborted turn was stopped on purpose.
526	    // Neither is a delivery failure, and reporting them as one puts a red error
527	    // on the conversation every time the user presses stop or steers again.
528	    if (isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error) || isWorkerTurnAbortedError(error)) {
529	      await releaseSupersededRow(appendedToTranscript);
530	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
531	      return;
532	    }
533	    await handleInterruptDeliveryError({ run, worker, record, userMessage, error, generation, source, requestedAt });
534	  }
535	}
536	
537	async function handleInterruptDeliveryError(args: {
538	  run: RunRecord;
539	  worker: WorkerRecord;
540	  record: QueuedRecord;
541	  userMessage: { id: string; createdAt: Date };
542	  error: unknown;
543	  generation: number;
544	  source: InterruptSource;
545	  requestedAt: number;
546	}) {
547	  const { run, worker, record, userMessage, error, generation, source, requestedAt } = args;
548	  const runId = run.id;
549	  const failedAt = new Date();
550	
551	  // A late failure from an interrupted (older) turn or a row the user cancelled
552	  // must not flip queue state.
553	  if (await queuedRowStatus(record.id) !== "delivering") {
554	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
555	    return;
556	  }
557	  if (!(await isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(worker.id, generation))) {
558	    const currentRecord = await db
559	      .select({ updatedAt: queuedConversationMessages.updatedAt })
560	      .from(queuedConversationMessages)
561	      .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id))
562	      .get();
563	    if (timestampMs(currentRecord?.updatedAt) > timestampMs(userMessage.createdAt)) {
564	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
565	      return;
566	    }
567	  }
568	
569	  const rawErrorMessage = errorMessage(error);
570	  const surfacedErrorMessage = userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(rawErrorMessage);
571	  const busy = isAgentBusyError(error);
572	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
573	    status: busy ? "pending" : "failed",
574	    lastError: surfacedErrorMessage,
575	    updatedAt: failedAt,
576	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
577	
578	  if (isEmptyQueuedWorkerOutputError(error)) {
579	    await db.update(workers).set({
580	      status: "error",
581	      outputLog: surfacedErrorMessage,
582	      updatedAt: failedAt,
583	    }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
584	    if (run.mode === "direct" || run.mode === "commit") {
585	      await persistRunFailure(runId, new Error(surfacedErrorMessage), {
586	        surface: { code: "worker.idle.empty_output", workerId: worker.id },
587	      });
588	    }
589	  }
590	
591	  if (busy) {
592	    await recordExecutionEvent({
593	      runId,
594	      workerId: worker.id,
595	      eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_deferred",
596	      details: {
597	        summary: `Worker ${worker.id} was still busy after cancel; queued message kept pending.`,
598	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
599	        error: surfacedErrorMessage,
600	        source,
601	      },
602	    });
603	  } else {
604	    await recordExecutionEvent({
605	      runId,
606	      workerId: worker.id,
607	      eventType: "queued_message_interrupt_failed",
608	      details: {
609	        summary: `Interrupt delivery failed for ${worker.id}.`,
610	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
611	        error: surfacedErrorMessage,
612	        source,
613	      },
614	    });
615	    emitNamedEvent({
616	      kind: "error.surfaced",
617	      code: "queue.interrupt.delivery_failed",
618	      message: `Interrupt delivery failed: ${surfacedErrorMessage}`,
619	      surface: "toast",
620	      runId,
621	      workerId: worker.id,
622	    });
623	  }
624	
625	  emitNamedEvent({
626	    kind: "queue.interrupt_delivery_failed",
627	    runId,
628	    workerId: worker.id,
629	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
630	    reason: busy ? "busy_after_cancel" : "delivery_failed",
631	    deferred: busy,
632	    totalInterruptLatencyMs: Date.now() - requestedAt,
633	    source,
634	  });
635	
636	  if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
637	    await recordExecutionEvent({
638	      runId,
639	      workerId: worker.id,
640	      eventType: "queued_message_recovery_blocked",
641	      details: {
642	        summary: `Queued message ${record.id} is blocked because ${worker.id} is missing.`,
643	        queuedMessageId: record.id,
644	        error: errorMessage(error),
645	        source,
646	      },
647	    });
648	    await reconcileRunRecovery({
649	      runId,
650	      liveAgents: [],
651	      source: "queued-message-interrupt",
652	    });
653	  }
654	
655	  // A busy delivery already removed nothing; non-recoverable failures keep the
656	  // appended worker-stream input for audit. Drop the duplicate checkpoint
657	  // message row only when we deferred so a later retry re-appends cleanly.
658	  if (busy) {
659	    await db.delete(messages).where(eq(messages.id, userMessage.id));
660	  }
661	
662	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
663	}
664	
665	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
666	// Entry points
667	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
668	
669	async function interruptAndSendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked(params: {
670	  runId: string;
671	  messageId: string;
672	  source?: InterruptSource;
673	}): Promise<InterruptResult> {
674	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(params.runId);
675	  const run = await loadRun(params.runId);
676	  const record = await loadQueuedRecord(params.runId, params.messageId);
677	  return interruptAndDeliver({ run, record, source: params.source ?? "drawer" });
678	}
679	
680	export function interruptAndSendQueuedConversationMessageNow(args: Parameters<typeof interruptAndSendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked>[0]) {
681	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => interruptAndSendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked(args));
682	}
683	
684	async function interruptAndSendNextQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked(params: {
685	  runId: string;
686	  source?: InterruptSource;
687	}): Promise<InterruptResult> {
688	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(params.runId);
689	  const run = await loadRun(params.runId);
690	  const record = await selectOldestPendingRecord(params.runId);
691	  return interruptAndDeliver({ run, record, source: params.source ?? "escape" });
692	}
693	
694	export function interruptAndSendNextQueuedConversationMessage(args: Parameters<typeof interruptAndSendNextQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked>[0]) {
695	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => interruptAndSendNextQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked(args));
696	}
697	
698	async function interruptWithDraftMessageUnlocked(params: {
699	  runId: string;
700	  content: string;
701	  attachments?: ChatAttachment[];
702	  targetWorkerId?: string | null;
703	  source?: InterruptSource;
704	}): Promise<InterruptResult> {
705	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(params.runId);
706	  const run = await loadRun(params.runId);
707	  const trimmed = params.content.trim();
708	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(params.attachments ?? []);
709	  if (!trimmed && normalizedAttachments.length === 0) {
710	    emitNamedEvent({
711	      kind: "queue.interrupt_refused",
712	      runId: params.runId,
713	      workerId: params.targetWorkerId ?? null,
714	      queuedMessageId: null,
715	      reason: "no_user_intent",
716	      source: params.source ?? "escape",
717	    });
718	    throw refusal(400, "Message content or attachment is required");
719	  }
720	
721	  // Persist the draft as exactly one queued row, then deliver that same id.
722	  const created = await createQueuedConversationMessage({
723	    runId: params.runId,
724	    targetWorkerId: params.targetWorkerId ?? null,
725	    action: "steer",
726	    content: params.content,
727	    attachments: normalizedAttachments,
728	  });
729	  const record = await loadQueuedRecord(params.runId, created.id);
730	  return interruptAndDeliver({ run, record, source: params.source ?? "escape" });
731	}
732	
733	export function interruptWithDraftMessage(args: Parameters<typeof interruptWithDraftMessageUnlocked>[0]) {
734	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => interruptWithDraftMessageUnlocked(args));
735	}

100	function timestampMs(value: Date | string | number | null | undefined) {
101	  if (value instanceof Date) {
102	    return value.getTime();
103	  }
104	  const parsed = new Date(value ?? 0).getTime();
105	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
106	}

108	async function nextQueuedMessageCreatedAt(runId: string) {
109	  const latest = await db
110	    .select({ createdAt: queuedConversationMessages.createdAt })
111	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
112	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
113	    .orderBy(desc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), desc(queuedConversationMessages.id))
114	    .get();
115	  const previousMs = Math.max(
116	    timestampMs(latest?.createdAt),
117	    lastQueuedMessageCreatedAtByRun.get(runId) ?? 0,
118	  );
119	  // Drizzle's SQLite timestamp mode stores integer seconds in this schema,
120	  // so use a one-second logical tick to preserve FIFO order through DB reads
121	  // that sort only by createdAt.
122	  const nextMs = Math.max(Date.now(), previousMs + 1_000);
123	  lastQueuedMessageCreatedAtByRun.set(runId, nextMs);
124	  return new Date(nextMs);
125	}

154	function workerStreamHasOutputAfterInput(
155	  entries: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof readWorkerOutputEntries>>,
156	  userInputEntryId: string,
157	) {

159	  if (!inputEntry) {

241	function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
242	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
243	    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
244	    : null;

247	function asString(value: unknown): string | null {

251	function asRequestedSchema(value: unknown): ElicitationSchema | null {
252	  const schema = asRecord(value);
253	  if (!schema) {
254	    return null;
255	  }
256	  const properties = asRecord(schema.properties) ?? {};
257	  const required = Array.isArray(schema.required)
258	    ? schema.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
259	    : undefined;
260	  const type = asString(schema.type);
261	  return {
262	    properties,
263	    ...(type ? { type } : {}),
264	    ...(required ? { required } : {}),
265	  };
266	}
267	
268	function isOpenElicitationEntry(entry: NonNullable<WorkerSnapshot["outputEntries"]>[number]) {
269	  if (entry.type !== "elicitation") {
270	    return false;
271	  }
272	  const status = (entry.status ?? "pending").trim().toLowerCase();
273	  return !["answered", "approved", "cancelled", "canceled", "completed", "declined", "denied", "failed", "rejected", "skipped"].includes(status);
274	}
275	
276	function pendingElicitationFromEntry(entry: NonNullable<WorkerSnapshot["outputEntries"]>[number]): PendingElicitation | null {
277	  if (!isOpenElicitationEntry(entry)) {
278	    return null;
279	  }
280	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
281	  const requestId = raw?.requestId;
282	  if (!raw || typeof requestId !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(requestId)) {
283	    return null;
284	  }
285	  return {
286	    requestId,
287	    requestedAt: entry.timestamp,
288	    sessionId: asString(raw.sessionId),
289	    toolCallId: asString(raw.toolCallId ?? entry.toolCallId),
290	    message: asString(raw.message),
291	    requestedSchema: asRequestedSchema(raw.requestedSchema),
292	  };
293	}
294	
295	function selectPendingWorkerElicitation(snapshot: WorkerSnapshot | null): PendingElicitation | null {
296	  const pending = snapshot?.pendingElicitations?.[0] ?? null;
297	  if (pending) {
298	    return pending;
299	  }
300	  const entries = snapshot?.outputEntries ?? [];
301	  for (const entry of entries) {
302	    const elicitation = pendingElicitationFromEntry(entry);
303	    if (elicitation) {
304	      return elicitation;
305	    }
306	  }
307	  return null;
308	}
309	
310	function elicitationAnswerContent(text: string, requestedSchema: ElicitationSchema | null | undefined): ElicitationContent {
311	  const properties = requestedSchema?.properties ?? {};
312	  const propertyNames = Object.keys(properties);
313	  if (propertyNames.includes("customAnswer")) {
314	    return { customAnswer: text };
315	  }
316	
317	  const nonCustomFields = propertyNames.filter((name) => name !== "customAnswer");
318	  if (nonCustomFields.length === 1 && nonCustomFields[0]) {
319	    return { [nonCustomFields[0]]: text };
320	  }
321	
322	  return { response: text };
323	}
324	
325	async function answerPendingWorkerElicitation(args: {
326	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
327	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
328	  snapshot: WorkerSnapshot | null;
329	  content: string;
330	  deliveredAt: Date;
331	}) {
332	  const elicitation = selectPendingWorkerElicitation(args.snapshot);
333	  if (!elicitation) {
334	    return false;
335	  }
336	
337	  try {
338	    await respondElicitation(args.worker.id, {
339	      action: "accept",
340	      content: elicitationAnswerContent(args.content, elicitation.requestedSchema),
341	    });
342	  } catch (error) {
343	    if (!isElicitationNoLongerPendingError(error)) {
344	      throw error;
345	    }
346	    // The question is gone, but the user's answer is not. Close the stale
347	    // stream row so the card stops advertising an open question, then report
348	    // "not delivered as an elicitation" so the caller falls through to normal
349	    // prompt delivery. Rethrowing here would fail the queued row and silently
350	    // discard everything the user typed.
351	    await closeStaleHumanInputEntries({
352	      workerId: args.worker.id,
353	      kind: "elicitation",
354	      requestId: elicitation.requestId,
355	      reason: "the worker stopped waiting for an answer",
356	    });
357	    return false;
358	  }
359	  await db.update(workers).set({
360	    status: "working",
361	    updatedAt: args.deliveredAt,
362	  }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
363	  await db.update(runs).set({
364	    status: "running",
365	    failedAt: null,
366	    lastError: null,
367	    updatedAt: args.deliveredAt,
368	  }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
369	  emitNamedEvent({
370	    kind: "worker.status",
371	    runId: args.run.id,
372	    workerId: args.worker.id,
373	    prev: args.worker.status,
374	    next: "working",
375	  });
376	  return true;
377	}

379	export async function persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({

381	  workerId,
382	  response,
383	  deliveredAt,
384	  userInputEntryId,

386	  run: WorkerResponseRun;

452	export async function listPendingQueuedConversationMessages(runId: string) {
453	  const records = await db
454	    .select()
455	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
456	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
457	    .orderBy(asc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), asc(queuedConversationMessages.id));
458	
459	  // `delivering` has left the queue: the message is with the worker and already
460	  // shown in the transcript. Failed dispatches return to `pending`.
461	  return records
462	    .filter((record) => record.status === "pending")
463	    .map(serializeQueuedConversationMessage);
464	}
465	
466	/**
467	 * `delivering` means "an in-process delivery owns this row". No such delivery
468	 * survives a process restart, so any row still marked `delivering` at boot is
469	 * orphaned: its owner died mid-flight. Left alone it dangles forever — and
470	 * since `delivering` rows are no longer listed as queued, it would dangle
471	 * invisibly. Reclaim them as `pending` so the user's message comes back.
472	 */
473	export async function reclaimOrphanedDeliveringMessages() {
474	  const orphaned = await db
475	    .select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id, runId: queuedConversationMessages.runId })
476	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
477	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "delivering"));
478	  if (orphaned.length === 0) {
479	    return 0;
480	  }
481	
482	  const now = new Date();
483	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
484	    status: "pending",
485	    lastError: null,
486	    updatedAt: now,
487	    deliveredAt: null,
488	  }).where(inArray(queuedConversationMessages.id, orphaned.map((record) => record.id)));
489	
490	  for (const record of orphaned) {
491	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(record.runId, "queued_message_reclaimed", {
492	      summary: "Requeued a message whose delivery was interrupted by a restart.",
493	      queuedMessageId: record.id,
494	    });
495	  }
496	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
497	  return orphaned.length;
498	}
499	
500	const CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
501	
502	/**
503	 * Falls back to a fresh uuid when the client id is unusable — malformed, or
504	 * already taken by an earlier queue row — so a resend degrades to a duplicate
505	 * row rather than a primary-key failure that would lose the user's text.
506	 */
507	async function resolveQueuedConversationMessageId(clientMessageId: string | null | undefined) {
508	  const candidate = typeof clientMessageId === "string" ? clientMessageId.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
509	  if (!CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN.test(candidate)) {
510	    return randomUUID();
511	  }
512	  const existing = await db
513	    .select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id })
514	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
515	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, candidate))
516	    .get();
517	  return existing ? randomUUID() : candidate;
518	}
519	
520	async function createQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked({
521	  runId,
522	  targetWorkerId = null,
523	  action,
524	  content,
525	  attachments = [],
526	  clientMessageId = null,
527	}: {
528	  runId: string;
529	  targetWorkerId?: string | null;
530	  action: BusyMessageAction;
531	  content: string;
532	  attachments?: ChatAttachment[];
533	  /**
534	   * Id the sending client already rendered its queue row under. Adopting it
535	   * keeps that row and this one the same row, so the queue entry does not
536	   * blink out and back when the event stream catches up.
537	   */
538	  clientMessageId?: string | null;
539	}) {
540	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
541	  const trimmedContent = content.trim();
542	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(attachments);
543	  if (!trimmedContent && normalizedAttachments.length === 0) {
544	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Message content or attachment is required"), { status: 400 });
545	  }
546	
547	  const now = await nextQueuedMessageCreatedAt(runId);
548	  const record = {
549	    id: await resolveQueuedConversationMessageId(clientMessageId),
550	    runId,
551	    targetWorkerId,
552	    action,
553	    content: trimmedContent,
554	    attachmentsJson: serializeChatAttachments(normalizedAttachments),
555	    status: "pending",
556	    lastError: null,
557	    createdAt: now,
558	    updatedAt: now,
559	    deliveredAt: null,
560	  };
561	
562	  await db.insert(queuedConversationMessages).values(record);
563	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_created", {
564	    summary: action === "steer" ? "Steering message was deferred into the queue." : "Message queued for the next safe turn.",
565	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
566	    action,
567	  }, targetWorkerId);
568	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
569	  return serializeQueuedConversationMessage(record);
570	}
571	
572	export function createQueuedConversationMessage(args: Parameters<typeof createQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked>[0]) {
573	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => createQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked(args));
574	}
575	
576	async function cancelQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked({
577	  runId,
578	  messageId,
579	}: {
580	  runId: string;
581	  messageId: string;
582	}) {
583	  const record = await db
584	    .select()
585	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
586	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
587	    .get();
588	
589	  if (!record || record.runId !== runId) {
590	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message not found"), { status: 404 });
591	  }
592	
593	  if (record.status === "delivered") {
594	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message was already delivered and cannot be cancelled"), { status: 409 });
595	  }
596	
597	  if (record.status === "cancelled") {
598	    return serializeQueuedConversationMessage(record);
599	  }
600	
601	  const now = new Date();
602	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
603	    status: "cancelled",
604	    updatedAt: now,
605	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
606	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_cancelled", {
607	    summary: "Cancelled queued message.",
608	    queuedMessageId: messageId,
609	  }, record.targetWorkerId);
610	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
611	
612	  return serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
613	    ...record,
614	    status: "cancelled",
615	    updatedAt: now,
616	  });
617	}

619	export function cancelQueuedConversationMessage(args: Parameters<typeof cancelQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked>[0]) {
620	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => cancelQueuedConversationMessageUnlocked(args));
621	}
622	
623	async function deliverQueuedWorkerSteering(args: {
624	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
625	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
626	  messageId: string;
627	  userMessageId?: string | null;
628	  content: string;
629	  userText: string;
630	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
631	  onUserInputAppended?: () => void;
632	}) {
633	  await runWorkerTurn(args.worker.id, async () => {
634	    const appendQueuedUserInput = async (deliveredAt: Date) => {
635	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
636	        id: args.userMessageId ?? args.messageId,
637	        runId: args.run.id,
638	        workerId: args.worker.id,
639	        text: args.userText,
640	        deliveredAt,
641	        attachments: args.attachments.map((attachment) => ({
642	          id: attachment.id,
643	          filename: attachment.name,
644	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
645	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
646	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
647	        })),
648	      });
649	      args.onUserInputAppended?.();
650	    };
651	
652	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
653	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
654	      return;
655	    }
656	
657	    const snapshotBeforeAsk = await Promise.resolve(getAgent(args.worker.id)).catch(() => null);
658	    const deliveredAt = new Date();
659	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
660	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
661	      return;
662	    }
663	    if (await answerPendingWorkerElicitation({
664	      run: args.run,
665	      worker: args.worker,
666	      snapshot: snapshotBeforeAsk,
667	      content: args.userText,
668	      deliveredAt,
669	    })) {
670	      await appendQueuedUserInput(deliveredAt);
671	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
672	        status: "delivered",
673	        lastError: null,
674	        updatedAt: deliveredAt,
675	        deliveredAt,
676	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
677	      await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_delivered", {
678	        summary: `Delivered queued answer to ${args.worker.id}'s pending question.`,
679	        queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
680	        action: "steer",
681	        delivery: "elicitation",
682	      }, args.worker.id);
683	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
684	      return;
685	    }
686	
687	    // Anchor the user's message before the ask: this is the user-initiated
688	    // send-now path, and the bridge starts streaming output during askAgent, so
689	    // appending afterwards would order the reply ahead of the prompt.
690	    await appendQueuedUserInput(deliveredAt);
691	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
692	
693	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
694	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
695	      return;
696	    }
697	
698	    const queuedPrompt = workerPromptForRun(args.run, args.content);
699	    const queuedImages = resolveImageAttachments(args.attachments, getAppDataPath);
700	    const response = queuedImages.length
701	      ? await askAgent(args.worker.id, queuedPrompt, queuedImages)
702	      : await askAgent(args.worker.id, queuedPrompt);
703	    await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
704	      run: args.run,
705	      workerId: args.worker.id,
706	      response,
707	      deliveredAt,
708	      userInputEntryId: args.userMessageId ?? args.messageId,
709	    });
710	    // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream; the
711	    // legacy role:"worker" messages row is no longer written.
712	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
713	      status: "delivered",
714	      lastError: null,
715	      updatedAt: deliveredAt,
716	      deliveredAt,
717	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
718	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_delivered", {
719	      summary: `Delivered immediate queued steering to ${args.worker.id}.`,
720	      queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
721	      action: "steer",
722	    }, args.worker.id);
723	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
724	  });
725	}
726	
727	async function continueQueuedWorkerSteering(args: {
728	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
729	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
730	  messageId: string;
731	  userMessageId?: string | null;
732	  content: string;
733	  userText: string;
734	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
735	}) {
736	  let userInputAppended = false;
737	  try {
738	    await deliverQueuedWorkerSteering({
739	      ...args,
740	      onUserInputAppended: () => {
741	        userInputAppended = true;
742	      },
743	    });
744	    return;
745	  } catch (error) {
746	    if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
747	      const fallbackWorker = await getLatestRunWorker(args.run.id, args.worker.id);
748	      if (fallbackWorker) {
749	        try {
750	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
751	            targetWorkerId: fallbackWorker.id,
752	            updatedAt: new Date(),
753	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
754	          await deliverQueuedWorkerSteering({
755	            ...args,
756	            worker: fallbackWorker,
757	            onUserInputAppended: () => {
758	              userInputAppended = true;
759	            },
760	          });
761	          return;
762	        } catch (fallbackError) {
763	          error = fallbackError;
764	        }
765	      }
766	    }
767	
768	    const failedAt = new Date();
769	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
770	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
771	      return;
772	    }
773	    if (await handleQueuedWorkerQuotaError({
774	      runId: args.run.id,
775	      workerId: args.worker.id,
776	      workerType: args.worker.type,
777	      error,
778	    })) {
779	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
780	        status: "pending",
781	        lastError: null,
782	        updatedAt: new Date(),
783	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
784	      return;
785	    }
786	
787	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
788	      status: isAgentBusyError(error) ? "pending" : "failed",
789	      lastError: errorMessage(error),
790	      updatedAt: failedAt,
791	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
792	    if (isEmptyQueuedWorkerOutputError(error)) {
793	      await db.update(workers).set({
794	        status: "error",
795	        outputLog: error.message,
796	        updatedAt: failedAt,
797	      }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
798	      if (args.run.mode === "direct" || args.run.mode === "commit") {
799	        await persistRunFailure(args.run.id, error, {
800	          surface: { code: "worker.idle.empty_output", workerId: args.worker.id },
801	        });
802	      }
803	    }
804	    if (args.userMessageId && (isAgentBusyError(error) || !userInputAppended)) {
805	      await db.delete(messages).where(eq(messages.id, args.userMessageId));
806	    }
807	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, isAgentBusyError(error) ? "queued_message_deferred" : "queued_message_failed", {
808	      summary: isAgentBusyError(error)
809	        ? `Worker ${args.worker.id} is still busy; queued message will be retried.`
810	        : `Immediate queued steering failed for ${args.worker.id}.`,
811	      queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
812	      action: "steer",
813	      error: errorMessage(error),
814	    }, args.worker.id);
815	    if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
816	      await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_recovery_blocked", {
817	        summary: `Queued message ${args.messageId} is blocked because ${args.worker.id} is missing.`,
818	        queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
819	        action: "steer",
820	        error: errorMessage(error),
821	      }, args.worker.id);
822	      await reconcileRunRecovery({
823	        runId: args.run.id,
824	        liveAgents: [],
825	        source: "queued-message-delivery",
826	      });
827	    }
828	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
829	  }
830	}
831	
832	async function sendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked({
833	  runId,
834	  messageId,
835	}: {
836	  runId: string;
837	  messageId: string;
838	}) {
839	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
840	  const record = await db
841	    .select()
842	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
843	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
844	    .get();
845	
846	  if (!record || record.runId !== runId || record.status !== "pending") {
847	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message not found"), { status: 404 });
848	  }
849	
850	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
851	  if (!run) {
852	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Conversation not found"), { status: 404 });
853	  }
854	
855	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []);
856	  const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
857	    resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
858	    imagesInlined: true,
859	  });
860	  const startedAt = new Date();
861	
862	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
863	    action: "steer",
864	    status: "delivering",
865	    lastError: null,
866	    updatedAt: startedAt,
867	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
868	
869	  if (run.mode === "implementation") {
870	    await db.update(runs).set({
871	      status: "running",
872	      failedAt: null,
873	      lastError: null,
874	      updatedAt: startedAt,
875	    }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
876	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
877	      action: "steer",
878	      status: "pending",
879	      lastError: null,
880	      updatedAt: startedAt,
881	      deliveredAt: null,
882	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
883	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_sent_now", {
884	      summary: "Accepted queued message for implementation worker steering.",
885	      queuedMessageId: messageId,
886	      action: "steer",
887	    });
888	    startSupervisorRun(runId);
889	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
890	
891	    return {
892	      ok: true,
893	      queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
894	        ...record,
895	        action: "steer",
896	        status: "pending",
897	        lastError: null,
898	        updatedAt: startedAt,
899	        deliveredAt: null,
900	      }),
901	    };
902	  }
903	
904	  const targetWorker = record.targetWorkerId
905	    ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, record.targetWorkerId)).get()
906	    : null;
907	  const worker = targetWorker && targetWorker.runId === runId
908	    ? targetWorker
909	    : await getLatestRunWorker(runId, record.targetWorkerId);
910	  if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId) {
911	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
912	      status: "pending",
913	      updatedAt: new Date(),
914	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
915	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Conversation worker not found"), { status: 404 });
916	  }
917	
918	  if (isWorkerClearlyBusy(worker)) {
919	    const error = `Ask failed: Agent is busy: ${worker.id}`;
920	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
921	      action: "steer",
922	      targetWorkerId: worker.id,
923	      status: "pending",
924	      lastError: error,
925	      updatedAt: new Date(),
926	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
927	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_deferred", {
928	      summary: `Worker ${worker.id} is still busy; queued message will be retried.`,
929	      queuedMessageId: messageId,
930	      action: "steer",
931	      error,
932	    }, worker.id);
933	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
934	
935	    return {
936	      ok: true,
937	      queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
938	        ...record,
939	        targetWorkerId: worker.id,
940	        action: "steer",
941	        status: "pending",
942	        lastError: error,
943	        updatedAt: startedAt,
944	        deliveredAt: null,
945	      }),
946	    };
947	  }
948	
949	  const userMessage = {
950	    id: messageId,
951	    runId,
952	    role: "user",
953	    kind: "checkpoint",
954	    content: record.content,
955	    attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
956	    createdAt: startedAt,
957	  };
958	
959	  await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
960	  await db.update(runs).set({
961	    status: run.mode === "planning" ? "working" : "running",
962	    failedAt: null,
963	    lastError: null,
964	    updatedAt: startedAt,
965	  }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
966	  await db.update(workers).set({
967	    status: "working",
968	    updatedAt: startedAt,
969	  }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
970	
971	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_sent_now", {
972	    summary: `Accepted queued message for immediate steering to ${worker.id}.`,
973	    queuedMessageId: messageId,
974	    action: "steer",
975	  }, worker.id);
976	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
977	
978	  trackConversationBackgroundTask(continueQueuedWorkerSteering({
979	    run,
980	    worker,
981	    messageId,
982	    userMessageId: userMessage.id,
983	    content: workerContent,
984	    userText: record.content,
985	    attachments: normalizedAttachments,
986	  }), { runId }).catch((error) => {
987	    console.error("Queued message immediate steering failed:", error);
988	  });
989	
990	  return {
991	    ok: true,
992	    message: serializeMessageRecord(userMessage),
993	    queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
994	      ...record,
995	      targetWorkerId: worker.id,
996	      action: "steer",
997	      status: "delivering",
998	      lastError: null,
999	      updatedAt: startedAt,
1000	      deliveredAt: null,
1001	    }),
1002	  };
1003	}
1004	
1005	export function sendQueuedConversationMessageNow(args: Parameters<typeof sendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked>[0]) {
1006	  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => sendQueuedConversationMessageNowUnlocked(args));
1007	}
1008	
1009	async function pendingQueueRecords(runId: string, workerId?: string | null) {
1010	  const records = await db
1011	    .select()
1012	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
1013	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
1014	    .orderBy(asc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), asc(queuedConversationMessages.id));
1015	
1016	  return records.filter((record) => {
1017	    if (record.status !== "pending") {
1018	      return false;
1019	    }
1020	
1021	    if (workerId === undefined) {
1022	      return record.targetWorkerId === null;
1023	    }
1024	
1025	    return record.targetWorkerId === workerId;
1026	  });
1027	}
1028	
1029	async function drainQueuedImplementationMessagesUnlocked(runId: string) {
1030	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
1031	  const records = await pendingQueueRecords(runId);
1032	  let deliveredCount = 0;
1033	
1034	  for (const record of records) {
1035	    const now = new Date();
1036	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1037	      status: "delivering",
1038	      updatedAt: now,
1039	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1040	
1041	    if (record.action === "steer") {
1042	      const targetWorker = record.targetWorkerId
1043	        ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, record.targetWorkerId)).get()
1044	        : null;
1045	      const worker = targetWorker && targetWorker.runId === runId && !isCancelledWorkerStatus(targetWorker.status)
1046	        ? targetWorker
1047	        : await getLatestRunWorker(runId, record.targetWorkerId);
1048	
1049	      if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId || isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker.status)) {
1050	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1051	          status: "pending",
1052	          lastError: "Conversation worker not found",
1053	          updatedAt: new Date(),
1054	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1055	        await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_deferred", {
1056	          summary: "No active implementation worker is available; queued steering will be retried.",
1057	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
1058	          action: "steer",
1059	          error: "Conversation worker not found",
1060	        });
1061	        continue;
1062	      }
1063	
1064	      const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []);
1065	      const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
1066	        resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
1067	      });
1068	      let interventionId: string | null = null;
1069	
1070	      try {
1071	        await runWorkerTurn(worker.id, async () => {
1072	          await db.update(workers).set({
1073	            status: "working",
1074	            updatedAt: now,
1075	          }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1076	          const intervention = await recordSupervisorIntervention({
1077	            runId,
1078	            workerId: worker.id,
1079	            prompt: workerContent,
1080	            summary: `Sent user steering to ${worker.id}`,
1081	            interventionType: "continue",
1082	          });
1083	          interventionId = intervention.id;
1084	          const steerImages = resolveImageAttachments(normalizedAttachments, getAppDataPath);
1085	          const response = steerImages.length
1086	            ? await askAgent(worker.id, workerContent, steerImages)
1087	            : await askAgent(worker.id, workerContent);
1088	          const deliveredAt = new Date();
1089	          const userMessage = {
1090	            id: randomUUID(),
1091	            runId,
1092	            role: "user" as const,
1093	            kind: "checkpoint" as const,
1094	            content: record.content,
1095	            attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
1096	            createdAt: record.createdAt,
1097	          };
1098	          await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
1099	            id: userMessage.id,
1100	            runId,
1101	            workerId: worker.id,
1102	            text: record.content,
1103	            deliveredAt,
1104	            attachments: normalizedAttachments.map((attachment) => ({
1105	              id: attachment.id,
1106	              filename: attachment.name,
1107	              mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
1108	              sizeBytes: attachment.size,
1109	              storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
1110	            })),
1111	          });
1112	          await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
1113	          await db.insert(messages).values({
1114	            id: randomUUID(),
1115	            runId,
1116	            role: "supervisor",
1117	            kind: "update",
1118	            content: `Got it. I sent that to ${formatWorkerLabel(worker)} and will keep watching the run.`,
1119	            attachmentsJson: null,
1120	            createdAt: deliveredAt,
1121	          });
1122	          await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
1123	            run: { id: runId, mode: "implementation" },
1124	            workerId: worker.id,
1125	            response,
1126	            deliveredAt,
1127	            userInputEntryId: userMessage.id,
1128	          });
1129	          // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream.
1130	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1131	            targetWorkerId: worker.id,
1132	            status: "delivered",
1133	            updatedAt: deliveredAt,
1134	            deliveredAt,
1135	            lastError: null,
1136	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1137	          await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_delivered", {
1138	            summary: `Delivered queued steering to ${worker.id}.`,
1139	            queuedMessageId: record.id,
1140	            action: "steer",
1141	          }, worker.id);
1142	        });
1143	        deliveredCount += 1;
1144	      } catch (error) {
1145	        if (await handleQueuedWorkerQuotaError({
1146	          runId,
1147	          workerId: worker.id,
1148	          workerType: worker.type,
1149	          error,
1150	        })) {
1151	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1152	            targetWorkerId: worker.id,
1153	            status: "pending",
1154	            lastError: null,
1155	            updatedAt: new Date(),
1156	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1157	          continue;
1158	        }
1159	
1160	        const failedAt = new Date();
1161	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1162	          targetWorkerId: worker.id,
1163	          status: isAgentBusyError(error) ? "pending" : "failed",
1164	          lastError: errorMessage(error),
1165	          updatedAt: failedAt,
1166	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1167	        if (isAgentBusyError(error) && interventionId) {
1168	          await db.update(supervisorInterventions).set({
1169	            summary: `Deferred user steering to ${worker.id}; worker is busy.`,

1171	        }
1172	        await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, isAgentBusyError(error) ? "queued_message_deferred" : "queued_message_failed", {
1173	          summary: isAgentBusyError(error)
1174	            ? `Worker ${worker.id} is still busy; queued steering will be retried.`
1175	            : `Queued steering delivery failed for ${worker.id}.`,
1176	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
1177	          action: "steer",
1178	          error: errorMessage(error),
1179	        }, worker.id);
1180	      }
1181	      continue;
1182	    }
1183	
1184	    await db.insert(messages).values({
1185	      id: randomUUID(),
1186	      runId,
1187	      role: "user" as const,
1188	      kind: "checkpoint" as const,
1189	      content: record.content,
1190	      attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
1191	      createdAt: record.createdAt,
1192	    });
1193	
1194	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1195	      status: "delivered",
1196	      updatedAt: now,
1197	      deliveredAt: now,
1198	      lastError: null,

1214	export function drainQueuedImplementationMessages(runId: string) {

1218	async function drainQueuedWorkerMessagesUnlocked({
1219	  runId,
1220	  workerId,
1221	  snapshot,
1222	}: {
1223	  runId: string;
1224	  workerId: string;
1225	  snapshot?: WorkerSnapshot | null;
1226	}) {
1227	  await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
1228	  const worker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, workerId)).get();
1229	  if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId) {
1230	    return 0;
1231	  }
1232	
1233	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
1234	  if (!run) {
1235	    return 0;
1236	  }
1237	
1238	  const records = await pendingQueueRecords(runId, workerId);
1239	  let deliveredCount = 0;
1240	
1241	  for (const record of records) {
1242	    const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []);
1243	    const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
1244	      resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
1245	      imagesInlined: true,
1246	    });
1247	    const startedAt = new Date();
1248	
1249	    // Keyed by the queue row id, matching the send-now path: the stream entry
1250	    // is anchored before the ask, so a deferred retry must land on the same id
1251	    // for `appendUserInputOnDelivery` to dedup instead of double-appending.
1252	    const userMessage = {
1253	      id: record.id,
1254	      runId,
1255	      role: "user" as const,
1256	      kind: "checkpoint" as const,
1257	      content: record.content,
1258	      attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
1259	      createdAt: startedAt,
1260	    };
1261	    const claimed = await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1262	      status: "delivering",
1263	      updatedAt: startedAt,
1264	      lastError: null,
1265	    }).where(and(
1266	      eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id),
1267	      eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "pending"),
1268	    )).returning({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id });
1269	    if (claimed.length === 0) {
1270	      continue;
1271	    }
1272	    await db.update(runs).set({
1273	      status: run.mode === "planning" ? "working" : "running",
1274	      failedAt: null,
1275	      lastError: null,
1276	      updatedAt: startedAt,
1277	    }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
1278	
1279	    // A pending elicitation belongs to the turn that is already running. Do
1280	    // not queue its answer behind that same turn: the turn cannot finish until
1281	    // the answer arrives, so acquiring the per-worker turn gate here creates a
1282	    // self-deadlock and leaves the queue row in `delivering` forever.
1283	    if (selectPendingWorkerElicitation(snapshot ?? null)) {
1284	      const deliveredAt = new Date();
1285	      const answered = await answerPendingWorkerElicitation({
1286	        run,
1287	        worker,
1288	        snapshot: snapshot ?? null,
1289	        content: record.content,
1290	        deliveredAt,
1291	      });
1292	      if (answered) {
1293	        await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
1294	          id: userMessage.id,
1295	          runId,
1296	          workerId,
1297	          text: record.content,
1298	          deliveredAt,
1299	          attachments: normalizedAttachments.map((attachment) => ({
1300	            id: attachment.id,
1301	            filename: attachment.name,
1302	            mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
1303	            sizeBytes: attachment.size,
1304	            storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
1305	          })),
1306	        });
1307	        await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
1308	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1309	          status: "delivered",
1310	          lastError: null,
1311	          updatedAt: deliveredAt,
1312	          deliveredAt,
1313	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1314	        await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_delivered", {
1315	          summary: `Delivered queued answer to ${workerId}'s pending question.`,
1316	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
1317	          delivery: "elicitation",
1318	        }, workerId);
1319	        deliveredCount += 1;
1320	        continue;
1321	      }
1322	    }
1323	
1324	    try {
1325	      await runWorkerTurn(workerId, async () => {
1326	        await db.update(workers).set({
1327	          status: "working",
1328	          updatedAt: startedAt,
1329	        }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
1330	        const snapshotBeforeAsk = snapshot ?? await Promise.resolve(getAgent(workerId)).catch(() => null);
1331	        const deliveredAt = new Date();
1332	        if (await answerPendingWorkerElicitation({
1333	          run,
1334	          worker,
1335	          snapshot: snapshotBeforeAsk,
1336	          content: record.content,
1337	          deliveredAt,
1338	        })) {
1339	          await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
1340	            id: userMessage.id,
1341	            runId,
1342	            workerId,
1343	            text: record.content,
1344	            deliveredAt,
1345	            attachments: normalizedAttachments.map((attachment) => ({
1346	              id: attachment.id,
1347	              filename: attachment.name,
1348	              mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
1349	              sizeBytes: attachment.size,
1350	              storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
1351	            })),
1352	          });
1353	          await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
1354	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1355	            status: "delivered",
1356	            lastError: null,
1357	            updatedAt: deliveredAt,
1358	            deliveredAt,
1359	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1360	          await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_delivered", {
1361	            summary: `Delivered queued answer to ${workerId}'s pending question.`,
1362	            queuedMessageId: record.id,
1363	            delivery: "elicitation",
1364	          }, workerId);
1365	          return;
1366	        }
1367	
1368	        // Anchor the user's message before the ask. The turn can block for its
1369	        // whole duration — indefinitely, if the agent raises an elicitation
1370	        // mid-turn — and appending only after `askAgent` resolves left the
1371	        // prompt, the streamed output and the question itself invisible for
1372	        // that entire window: the conversation looked finished while the
1373	        // status said working. The queue row is already claimed `delivering`
1374	        // above, so no concurrent drain can re-enter this record, and the
1375	        // entry id is the queue row id, so a deferred retry dedups rather
1376	        // than appending the prompt twice.
1377	        await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
1378	          id: userMessage.id,
1379	          runId,
1380	          workerId,
1381	          text: record.content,
1382	          deliveredAt,
1383	          attachments: normalizedAttachments.map((attachment) => ({
1384	            id: attachment.id,
1385	            filename: attachment.name,
1386	            mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
1387	            sizeBytes: attachment.size,
1388	            storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
1389	          })),
1390	        });
1391	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1392	
1393	        const drainImages = resolveImageAttachments(normalizedAttachments, getAppDataPath);
1394	        const response = drainImages.length
1395	          ? await askAgent(workerId, workerPromptForRun(run, workerContent), drainImages)
1396	          : await askAgent(workerId, workerPromptForRun(run, workerContent));
1397	        await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
1398	        await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
1399	          run,
1400	          workerId,
1401	          response,
1402	          deliveredAt,
1403	          userInputEntryId: userMessage.id,
1404	        });
1405	        // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream.
1406	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1407	          status: "delivered",
1408	          lastError: null,
1409	          updatedAt: deliveredAt,
1410	          deliveredAt,
1411	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1412	        await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_delivered", {
1413	          summary: `Delivered queued message to ${workerId}.`,
1414	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
1415	        }, workerId);
1416	      });
1417	      deliveredCount += 1;
1418	    } catch (error) {
1419	      if (await handleQueuedWorkerQuotaError({
1420	        runId,
1421	        workerId,
1422	        workerType: worker.type,
1423	        error,
1424	      })) {
1425	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1426	          status: "pending",
1427	          lastError: null,
1428	          updatedAt: new Date(),
1429	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1430	        continue;
1431	      }
1432	
1433	      const failedAt = new Date();
1434	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1435	        status: isAgentBusyError(error) ? "pending" : "failed",
1436	        lastError: errorMessage(error),
1437	        updatedAt: failedAt,
1438	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1439	      await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, isAgentBusyError(error) ? "queued_message_deferred" : "queued_message_failed", {
1440	        summary: isAgentBusyError(error)
1441	          ? `Worker ${workerId} is still busy; queued message will be retried.`

1444	        error: errorMessage(error),

1447	      if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {

1468	  if (deliveredCount > 0 || records.length > 0) {

1472	  return deliveredCount;
1473	}

67	  for (const event of verificationEvents) {

69	    let details: { accountId?: unknown; liveness?: unknown; detail?: unknown };
70	    try {
71	      details = JSON.parse(event.details) as typeof details;
72	    } catch {
73	      continue;
74	    }
75	    if (details.liveness !== "dead" && details.liveness !== "live") continue;

321	async function recordQueueDrainDecision(args: {
322	  runId: string;

329	}) {
330	  emitNamedEvent({
331	    kind: "queue.drain_decision",
332	    runId: args.runId,
333	    workerId: args.workerId,

335	    workerStatus: args.workerStatus,
336	    pendingCount: args.pendingCount,
337	    decision: args.decision,
338	    reason: args.reason,
339	  });
340	  await recordExecutionEvent({

342	    workerId: args.workerId,
343	    eventType: "queue_drain_decision",
344	    details: {

346	        ? `Draining ${args.pendingCount} queued message(s) for ${args.workerId}.`
347	        : `Skipped queue drain for ${args.workerId}: ${args.reason}.`,
348	      source: args.source,

350	      pendingCount: args.pendingCount,

357	async function drainQueuedWorkerMessagesWithObservation(args: {
358	  runId: string;
359	  workerId: string;
360	  workerStatus: string;
361	  source: string;
362	  snapshot?: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord> | null;
363	}) {
364	  const pendingCount = await pendingWorkerQueueCount(args.runId, args.workerId);
365	  if (pendingCount === 0) {

371	  const drainable = hasPendingElicitation || isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(args.workerStatus);

378	      : drainable
379	        ? "worker_drainable"
380	        : "worker_not_drainable",

382	  if (!drainable) {

386	  // Deliver on a background task rather than inline. Sync passes are
387	  // serialized through `liveSyncQueue`, and the drain runs a full worker turn:
388	  // awaiting it here parked the entire sync chain for the length of the turn.
389	  // When the agent raised an elicitation mid-turn that became a deadlock — the
390	  // turn cannot finish until the question is answered, and the question is
391	  // only surfaced by the snapshot writes in the very sync pass that is stuck

394	  // return above instead of double-delivering.
395	  const delivery = trackConversationBackgroundTask((async () => {

398	      workerId: args.workerId,
399	      snapshot,

401	    emitNamedEvent({

404	      workerId: args.workerId,

406	      pendingCount,
407	      deliveredCount,
408	    });
409	    await recordExecutionEvent({
410	      runId: args.runId,
411	      workerId: args.workerId,
412	      eventType: "queue_drain_finished",
413	      details: {
414	        summary: `Queue drain finished for ${args.workerId}: delivered ${deliveredCount} of ${pendingCount}.`,
415	        source: args.source,
416	        pendingCount,
417	        deliveredCount,
418	      },
419	    });
420	    return deliveredCount;
421	  })(), { runId: args.runId });
422	  delivery.catch((error) => {
423	    console.error(`Queued message drain failed for ${args.workerId}:`, error);
424	  });
425	  return 0;
426	}
