```text
470	
471	function compareWorkersForFollowUp(a: typeof workers.$inferSelect, b: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
472	  const workerNumberDiff = (b.workerNumber ?? 0) - (a.workerNumber ?? 0);
473	  if (workerNumberDiff !== 0) {
474	    return workerNumberDiff;
475	  }
476	
477	  const createdAtDiff = workerCreatedAtMs(b) - workerCreatedAtMs(a);
478	  if (createdAtDiff !== 0) {
479	    return createdAtDiff;
480	  }
481	
482	  return b.id.localeCompare(a.id);
483	}
484	
485	function selectConversationWorker(runId: string, allWorkers: Array<typeof workers.$inferSelect>) {
486	  const sortedWorkers = allWorkers
487	    .filter((candidate) => candidate.runId === runId)
488	    .sort(compareWorkersForFollowUp);
489	  return sortedWorkers.find((worker) => !isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker.status)) ?? sortedWorkers[0] ?? null;
490	}
491	
492	async function clearMatchingRunFailureMessage(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
493	  if (!run.lastError) {
494	    return;
495	  }
496	
497	  await db.delete(messages).where(and(
498	    eq(messages.runId, run.id),
499	    eq(messages.role, "system"),
500	    eq(messages.kind, "error"),
501	    eq(messages.content, `Run failed: ${run.lastError}`),
502	  ));
503	}
504	
505	let bridgeSyncInFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;
506	
507	/**
508	 * Watchdog-driven reconciliation between the bridge runtime and persisted
509	 * worker/run state. Turn completions are normally persisted by the awaiting
510	 * send-message call, or by the SSE-driven sync that runs while a client is
511	 * connected — but both die with their in-flight request (server restart,
512	 * dropped ACP roundtrip, phone backgrounded the tab). A worker then sits at
513	 * status='working' forever while the bridge agent finished its turn long
514	 * ago, and the stuck-worker reaper keeps cancelling and re-delivering the
515	 * same prompt every timeout window. This sweep closes the gap from the 15s
516	 * watchdog without requiring any connected client.
517	 */
518	export function syncConversationSessionsFromBridge(): Promise<void> {
519	  if (bridgeSyncInFlight) {
520	    return bridgeSyncInFlight;
521	  }
522	
523	  bridgeSyncInFlight = (async () => {
524	    let rawAgents: unknown[];
525	    try {
526	      rawAgents = await listAgents({ retryIndefinitely: false });
527	    } catch {
528	      // Bridge down or unreachable — there is no live state to reconcile
529	      // against; the next sweep will retry.
530	      return;
531	    }
532	    await syncConversationSessions(rawAgents);
533	  })().finally(() => {
534	    bridgeSyncInFlight = null;
535	  });
536	  return bridgeSyncInFlight;
537	}
538	
539	type SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {
540	  selectedRunId?: string | null;
541	  refreshPlanningArtifacts?: boolean;
542	};
543	
544	let liveSyncQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
545	
546	export function __resetSyncConversationSessionsQueueForTests() {
547	  liveSyncQueue = Promise.resolve();
548	}
549	
550	export async function syncConversationSessions(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {
551	  const runSync = () => syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents, options);
552	  const nextSync = liveSyncQueue.then(runSync, runSync);
553	  liveSyncQueue = nextSync.catch(() => undefined);
554	  return nextSync;
555	}
556	
557	async function syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {
558	  const agents = rawAgents.map((agent) => normalizeAgentRecord(agent));
559	  const selectedRunId = options.selectedRunId?.trim() || null;
560	  const refreshPlanningArtifacts = options.refreshPlanningArtifacts !== false;
561	  const allRuns = selectedRunId
562	    ? await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, selectedRunId))
563	    : await db.select().from(runs);
564	  const allWorkers = selectedRunId
565	    ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, selectedRunId))
566	    : await db.select().from(workers);
567	  const activeQuotaIncidents = (selectedRunId
568	    ? await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, selectedRunId))
569	    : await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents)
570	  ).filter((incident) => (
571	    incident.kind === "quota_exhausted"
572	    && (incident.status === "open" || incident.status === "recovering")
573	  ));

575	  for (const run of allRuns) {
576	    // A user cancellation is authoritative. The bridge can report the old
577	    // turn as working until cancellation reaches the provider, but that late
578	    // snapshot must not reopen recovery or revive the conversation.
579	    if (isCancelledWorkerStatus(run.status)) {
580	      continue;
581	    }
582	
583	    const quotaIncident = activeQuotaIncidents.find((incident) => incident.runId === run.id);
584	    const quotaWorker = allWorkers.find((candidate) => candidate.id === quotaIncident?.workerId);
585	    const quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState = Boolean(
586	      quotaIncident
587	      && (
588	        run.status === "quota_waiting"
589	        || normalizedStatus(quotaWorker?.status) === "cred-exhausted"
590	      ),
591	    );
592	    if (quotaIncident && quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState) {
593	      if (run.status !== "quota_waiting" || run.lastError || run.failedAt) {
594	        const updated = await withSqliteBusyRetry(() => db.update(runs).set({
595	          status: "quota_waiting",
596	          failedAt: null,
597	          lastError: null,
598	          updatedAt: new Date(),
599	        }).where(and(

601	          eq(runs.status, run.status),
602	        )).returning({ id: runs.id }));

605	            kind: "recovery.quota_wait_preserved",

630	    if (run.mode === "implementation") {

632	      let syncedActiveLiveWorker = false;

634	      for (const implementationWorker of implementationWorkers) {

637	          implementationAgent
638	          && implementationWorker.bridgeSessionId

648	          continue;

651	        await writeWorkerOutputEntries(run.id, implementationWorker.id, implementationAgent.outputEntries);
652	        await withSqliteBusyRetry(() => db.update(workers).set({
653	          status: implementationAgent.state,
654	          cwd: implementationAgent.cwd || implementationWorker.cwd,
655	          currentText: implementationAgent.currentText,
656	          lastText: implementationAgent.lastText,

658	          bridgeSessionMode: implementationAgent.sessionMode ?? implementationWorker.bridgeSessionMode,

679	        continue;

759	      await persistRunFailure(run.id, new Error(EMPTY_IDLE_WORKER_OUTPUT_DIAGNOSTIC), {

761	      });

764	
765	    await writeWorkerOutputEntries(run.id, worker.id, agent.outputEntries);
766	    const nextRunState = resolveSyncedRunState(run, agent);
767	    const quiescedDirectWorker = isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && directLiveAgentHasCompletedTurn(agent);
768	    const nextWorkerStatus = quiescedDirectWorker ? "idle" : agent.state;
769	    const nextWorkerCwd = agent.cwd || worker.cwd;
770	    const nextWorkerCurrentText = quiescedDirectWorker ? "" : agent.currentText;
771	    const workerChanged = worker.status !== nextWorkerStatus
772	      || worker.cwd !== nextWorkerCwd
773	      || worker.currentText !== nextWorkerCurrentText
774	      || worker.lastText !== agent.lastText;
775	    const workerUpdatedAt = new Date();
776	    if (workerChanged) {
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Shared bytes-level append-only artifact storage.
3	 *
4	 * Modelled on the worker output-store JSONL+gzip+lock pattern. Every
5	 * stream is identified by (runId, kind, ownerId) and lives at a
6	 * stream-relative path inside the run's artifact root. The bytes layer
7	 * knows about:
8	 *
9	 *   - JSONL append with a guard for crash-truncated tail lines
10	 *   - Per-stream serialization via an in-process write chain + an
11	 *     OS-visible lock directory (covers dev process restarts and
12	 *     parallel module instances)
13	 *   - Tail-scan reading with a parameterized line predicate so callers
14	 *     can terminate early on a cursor boundary
15	 *   - Gzip compaction of inactive streams to `<stream>.jsonl.gz`
16	 *   - Expanding `.jsonl.gz` back to plaintext on a fresh append
17	 *   - Read-side `(size, mtimeMs)` cache invariants so unchanged files
18	 *     return without I/O on repeat polls
19	 *
20	 * Domain semantics — entry dedup, history truncation, legacy DB
21	 * fallbacks, archive zips, schema-level normalization — live in the
22	 * per-domain adapter (e.g. worker output-store). This module is
23	 * deliberately ignorant of them.
24	 */
25	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
26	import { promises as fs, existsSync } from "node:fs";
27	import path from "node:path";
28	import { gzipSync, gunzipSync } from "node:zlib";
29	import {
30	  resolveArtifactRoot,
31	  resolveStreamPathWithin,
32	  type ArtifactRoot,
33	} from "./project-root";
34	import {
35	  ARTIFACT_STREAM_OWNER_NONE,
36	  normalizeArtifactOwnerId,
37	  type ArtifactStreamId,
38	} from "./stream-types";
39	import {
40	  appendIndexEntry,
41	  deleteIndex,
42	  findIndexPointForSeq,
43	  readIndex,
44	  rebuildIndex,
45	  shouldIndex,
46	} from "./stream-index";
47	
48	// --- Tunables --------------------------------------------------------
49	
50	const FILE_LOCK_STALE_MS = 30_000;
51	const FILE_LOCK_UPDATE_MS = 5_000;
52	const FILE_LOCK_RETRIES = 20;
53	const FILE_LOCK_MIN_TIMEOUT_MS = 25;
54	const FILE_LOCK_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS = 250;
55	const FILE_LOCK_FACTOR = 1.2;
56	
57	// --- Public types ----------------------------------------------------
58	
59	export interface ArtifactStreamLocation {
60	  /** Stream identity as stored in SQLite metadata. */
61	  id: ArtifactStreamId;
62	  /** Resolved artifact root (project-local or legacy global fallback). */
63	  root: ArtifactRoot;
64	  /**
65	   * Stream path *relative to the artifact root*. Stored in
66	   * `artifact_streams.relative_path`. Forward-slash separated for
67	   * portability across OSes that store the same SQLite file.
68	   */
69	  relativeStreamPath: string;
70	  /** Absolute path of the plaintext `.jsonl` file. */
71	  filePath: string;
72	  /** Absolute path of the gzipped variant `<stream>.jsonl.gz`. */
73	  compressedFilePath: string;
74	  /** Absolute path of the lock directory. */
75	  lockPath: string;
76	}
77	
78	export interface AppendOptions {
79	  /** ID used to make the append idempotent. Re-appending the same id is a no-op. */
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Resolves the artifact root for a run.
3	 *
4	 * Canonical location: `<run.projectPath>/.omniharness/run-data/<runId>/`
5	 *
6	 * Runs without `projectPath` (legacy data created before project paths
7	 * were tracked) fall back to the app-global `<appData>/run-data/<runId>/`
8	 * for *reads only*. New writes against a run with no project path are
9	 * rejected with a stable error code — callers must surface this to the
10	 * user rather than silently re-creating the legacy global mess.
11	 *
12	 * Stored SQLite metadata records the relative path under `.omniharness/run-data`
13	 * plus the absolute `projectPath`, never an absolute artifact path.
14	 * That keeps the data portable when a project moves on disk (e.g. the
15	 * user renames `~/work/foo` → `~/work/bar`).
16	 */
17	import path from "node:path";
18	import { promises as fs } from "node:fs";
19	import { getAppDataPath } from "@/server/app-root";
20	import { ensureProjectOmniharnessDir, getProjectOmniharnessDir } from "@/server/projects/config";
21	
22	const RUN_DATA_SUBDIR = "run-data";
23	const OMNIHARNESS_DIR = ".omniharness";
24	
25	export class ArtifactRootError extends Error {
26	  readonly code: string;
27	  constructor(message: string, code: string) {
28	    super(message);
29	    this.code = code;
30	    this.name = "ArtifactRootError";
31	  }
32	}
33	
34	export type ArtifactRootSource = "project" | "legacy_global";
35	
36	export interface ArtifactRoot {
37	  /** Absolute path of the run's artifact root directory. */
38	  absolutePath: string;
39	  /** Whether the root is project-local or the legacy global fallback. */
40	  source: ArtifactRootSource;
41	  /**
42	   * The `projectPath` recorded in SQLite metadata when writing artifacts.
43	   * null for legacy global roots (no owning project).
44	   */
45	  projectPath: string | null;
46	  /**
47	   * Relative path *of the run's artifact root* under either
48	   * `<project>/.omniharness/run-data/` or `<appData>/run-data/`. Stored
49	   * in SQLite metadata to keep records portable.
50	   */
51	  relativeRootPath: string;
52	}
53	
54	/**
55	 * Resolve the artifact root for a run from server-owned run metadata.
56	 * `projectPath` here is the value already on `runs.projectPath`; callers
57	 * MUST NOT accept it directly from client input.
58	 *
59	 * `mode`:
60	 *   - `read`: returns a project-local root if available, else falls back
61	 *     to the legacy global root (without creating anything).
62	 *   - `write`: refuses to resolve a legacy-global root. New writes must
63	 *     have an owning project. Returns project-local root and `mkdir -p`s
64	 *     it as a side effect.
65	 */
66	export async function resolveArtifactRoot(
67	  args: { runId: string; projectPath: string | null | undefined },
68	  mode: "read" | "write",
69	): Promise<ArtifactRoot> {
70	  const runId = args.runId?.trim();
71	  if (!runId) {
72	    throw new ArtifactRootError("Run id is required to resolve artifact root.", "runid_missing");
73	  }
74	
75	  const projectPath = args.projectPath?.trim() || null;
76	
77	  if (projectPath) {
78	    assertSafeRunId(runId);
79	    const projectRoot = path.resolve(projectPath);
80	    const omniDir = getProjectOmniharnessDir(projectRoot);
81	    const runRoot = path.join(omniDir, RUN_DATA_SUBDIR, runId);
82	
83	    if (mode === "write") {
84	      // If the project directory itself has gone (renamed, deleted, or
85	      // an in-test fake path), fall through to the legacy global root
86	      // rather than fail the whole append. The repair tool/backfill can
87	      // relocate once the project is back. We DON'T retry/create the
88	      // missing project root — that would silently scatter artifacts
89	      // through unrelated filesystems on misconfigured runs.
90	      try {
91	        ensureProjectOmniharnessDir(projectRoot);
92	        await fs.mkdir(runRoot, { recursive: true });
93	        return {
94	          absolutePath: runRoot,
95	          source: "project",
96	          projectPath: projectRoot,
97	          relativeRootPath: path.posix.join(RUN_DATA_SUBDIR, runId),
98	        };
99	      } catch (error) {
100	        const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
101	        if (code !== "ENOENT" && code !== "ENOTDIR") {
102	          throw error;
103	        }
104	        // fall through to legacy below
105	      }
106	    } else {
107	      return {
108	        absolutePath: runRoot,
109	        source: "project",
110	        projectPath: projectRoot,
111	        relativeRootPath: path.posix.join(RUN_DATA_SUBDIR, runId),
112	      };
113	    }
114	  }
115	
116	  // Legacy fallback: runs created before projectPath was a required
117	  // attribute land here. We let them keep writing to the app-global
118	  // run-data directory rather than fail the whole feature for old data.
119	  // Backfill can relocate them later. New runs SHOULD have projectPath
120	  // set so they end up project-local from the start.
121	  const legacyRoot = legacyGlobalRunRoot(runId);
122	  if (mode === "write") {
123	    await fs.mkdir(legacyRoot, { recursive: true });
124	  }
125	  return {
126	    absolutePath: legacyRoot,
127	    source: "legacy_global",
128	    projectPath: null,
129	    relativeRootPath: path.posix.join(RUN_DATA_SUBDIR, runId),
130	  };
131	}
132	
133	/**
134	 * Returns the absolute path of an artifact stream given the run's root
135	 * and a stream-relative path. The stream-relative path is what's stored
136	 * in `artifact_streams.relative_path` (e.g. `workers/abc-worker-1.jsonl`
137	 * or `execution-events.jsonl`). Hard-fails on path traversal or paths
138	 * that escape the artifact root.
139	 */
140	export function resolveStreamPathWithin(root: ArtifactRoot, relativeStreamPath: string): string {
141	  const normalized = path.posix.normalize(relativeStreamPath).replace(/^[/\\]+/, "");
142	  if (normalized.startsWith("..") || path.isAbsolute(normalized) || normalized.includes("\0")) {
143	    throw new ArtifactRootError(
144	      `Refusing artifact path that escapes its root: ${relativeStreamPath}`,
145	      "path_traversal",
146	    );
147	  }
148	  const absolute = path.join(root.absolutePath, normalized);
149	  // Belt-and-braces: ensure the resolved absolute path is still inside
150	  // the root after normalization.
151	  const rootResolved = path.resolve(root.absolutePath);
152	  const absResolved = path.resolve(absolute);
153	  if (absResolved !== rootResolved && !absResolved.startsWith(rootResolved + path.sep)) {
154	    throw new ArtifactRootError(
155	      `Refusing artifact path that escapes its root: ${relativeStreamPath}`,
156	      "path_traversal",
157	    );
158	  }
159	  return absResolved;
160	}
161	
162	function legacyGlobalRunRoot(runId: string) {
163	  return path.join(getAppDataPath(RUN_DATA_SUBDIR), runId);
164	}
165	
166	/**
167	 * The legacy global artifact root for a run. Used by readers that still
168	 * need to fall back to pre-migration files. New writes MUST NOT land here.
169	 */
170	export function legacyGlobalArtifactRoot(runId: string): ArtifactRoot {
171	  return {
172	    absolutePath: legacyGlobalRunRoot(runId),
173	    source: "legacy_global",
174	    projectPath: null,
175	    relativeRootPath: path.posix.join(RUN_DATA_SUBDIR, runId),
176	  };
177	}
178	
179	function assertSafeRunId(runId: string) {
180	  if (runId.includes("/") || runId.includes("\\") || runId.includes("..") || runId.includes("\0")) {
181	    throw new ArtifactRootError(
182	      `Refusing artifact root for unsafe run id: ${JSON.stringify(runId)}`,
183	      "unsafe_runid",
184	    );
185	  }
186	}
187	
188	export const ARTIFACT_DIR_NAME = OMNIHARNESS_DIR;
189	export const ARTIFACT_RUN_DATA_SUBDIR = RUN_DATA_SUBDIR;
190
```

```text
4	function isPathInside(candidate: string, parent: string) {
5	  const relative = path.relative(parent, candidate);
6	  return relative !== "" && relative !== ".." && !relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) && !path.isAbsolute(relative);
7	}
8	
9	function isTestProcess() {

11	    || process.env.VITEST_WORKER_ID !== undefined
12	    || process.env.TEST_WORKER_INDEX !== undefined;
13	}
14	
15	function assertSafeTestRoot(root: string) {
16	  if (!isTestProcess()) {
17	    return;
18	  }
19	
20	  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);
21	  const resolvedCwd = path.resolve(process.cwd());
22	  const temporaryParents = [os.tmpdir(), "/tmp", "/var/tmp"].map((entry) => path.resolve(entry));
23	  const isTemporary = temporaryParents.some((parent) => isPathInside(resolvedRoot, parent));
24	
25	  if (resolvedRoot === resolvedCwd || !isTemporary) {
26	    throw new Error(
27	      `[database-safety] Refusing to use non-temporary app data root in a test process: ${resolvedRoot}`,
28	    );
29	  }
30	}
31	
32	export function getAppRoot() {
33	  const configuredRoot = process.env.OMNIHARNESS_ROOT?.trim();
34	  const root = configuredRoot ? path.resolve(configuredRoot) : process.cwd();
35	  assertSafeTestRoot(root);
36	  return root;
37	}
38	
39	export function getAppDataPath(...segments: string[]) {
40	  return path.join(getAppRoot(), ...segments);
```

```text
1	import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
2	import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
3	import { db } from "@/server/db";
4	import { workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
5	import { assertRunNotHandoffFenced } from "@/server/handoff/fence";
6	import { withRunWorkspaceMutationAdmission } from "@/server/handoff/workspace-lock";
7	
8	const workerTurnChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
9	const conversationMutationChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
10	const activeConversationMutation = new AsyncLocalStorage<string>();
11	const backgroundTasks = new Set<Promise<void>>();
12	const backgroundTasksByRunId = new Map<string, Set<Promise<void>>>();
13	const conversationDeletionRequests = new Set<string>();
14	const completedConversationDeletionRequests = new Set<string>();
15	
16	export function isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error: unknown): boolean {
17	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
18	  return /\bworker turn (?:was )?superseded by a newer worker turn\b/i.test(message);
19	}
20	
21	function runOnChain<T>(
22	  chains: Map<string, Promise<void>>,
23	  key: string,
24	  task: () => Promise<T>,
25	): Promise<T> {
26	  const previous = chains.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve();
27	  const next = previous.then(() => task());
28	  const tracked = next.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
29	  chains.set(key, tracked);
30	  return next.finally(() => {
31	    if (chains.get(key) === tracked) {
32	      chains.delete(key);
33	    }
34	  });
35	}
36	
37	/**
38	 * Stop and steer must be instant. Before this existed, neither could actually
39	 * end a running turn: `askAgent` read its SSE stream to completion with no
40	 * abort path, so a cancel only *asked* the adapter to stop and then waited for
41	 * it, and the next turn sat behind the dead one on the FIFO chain.
42	 *
43	 * Every turn now runs with an AbortSignal published on this async-local store,
44	 * so anything the turn calls — however deeply nested — can pick it up without
45	 * threading a parameter through every call site. `abortWorkerTurn` trips it
46	 * synchronously, which tears down the in-flight fetch locally rather than
47	 * negotiating with an agent that may be wedged.
48	 */
49	const workerTurnSignals = new AsyncLocalStorage<AbortSignal>();
50	const workerTurnAborts = new Map<string, AbortController>();
51	
52	export class WorkerTurnAbortedError extends Error {
53	  readonly workerId: string;
54	
55	  constructor(workerId: string, reason?: string) {
56	    super(`Worker turn aborted for ${workerId}${reason ? `: ${reason}` : ""}`);
57	    this.name = "WorkerTurnAbortedError";
58	    this.workerId = workerId;
59	  }
60	}
61	
62	export function isWorkerTurnAbortedError(error: unknown): boolean {
63	  if (error instanceof WorkerTurnAbortedError) {
64	    return true;
65	  }
66	  const name = (error as { name?: unknown } | null)?.name;
67	  if (name === "AbortError" || name === "WorkerTurnAbortedError") {
68	    return true;
69	  }
70	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error ?? "");
71	  return /\bworker turn aborted\b|\bthis operation was aborted\b/i.test(message);
72	}
73	
74	/** The AbortSignal of the turn running on this async context, if any. */
75	export function currentWorkerTurnSignal(): AbortSignal | undefined {
76	  return workerTurnSignals.getStore();
77	}
78	
79	export function runWorkerTurn<T>(workerId: string, task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
80	  return runOnChain(workerTurnChains, workerId, async () => {
81	    const controller = new AbortController();
82	    workerTurnAborts.set(workerId, controller);
83	    try {
84	      return await workerTurnSignals.run(controller.signal, () => task(controller.signal));
85	    } finally {
86	      if (workerTurnAborts.get(workerId) === controller) {
87	        workerTurnAborts.delete(workerId);
88	      }
89	    }
90	  });
91	}
92	
93	/**
94	 * Trip the running turn's signal for `workerId`. Synchronous and local: it does
95	 * not talk to the agent runtime, so a wedged adapter cannot delay it. Returns
96	 * false when there was no live turn to abort.
97	 */
98	export function abortWorkerTurn(workerId: string, reason?: string): boolean {
99	  const controller = workerTurnAborts.get(workerId);
100	  if (!controller || controller.signal.aborted) {
101	    return false;
102	  }
103	  controller.abort(new WorkerTurnAbortedError(workerId, reason));
104	  return true;
105	}
106	
107	export function hasLiveWorkerTurn(workerId: string): boolean {
108	  const controller = workerTurnAborts.get(workerId);
109	  return Boolean(controller && !controller.signal.aborted);
110	}
111	
112	export function __resetWorkerTurnAbortsForTests() {
113	  workerTurnAborts.clear();
114	}
115	
116	/**
117	 * Read the worker's current turn fence value. Returns 0 when the worker
118	 * row is missing (treated as the default generation).
119	 */
120	export async function readWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId: string): Promise<number> {
121	  const record = await db
122	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
123	    .from(workers)
124	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
125	    .get();
126	  return record?.turnGeneration ?? 0;
127	}
128	
129	/**
130	 * Capture a worker turn generation when the worker exists. A null result means
131	 * the bridge request is not backed by a persisted conversation worker and
132	 * therefore cannot participate in the database turn fence.
133	 */
134	export async function captureWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId: string): Promise<number | null> {
135	  const record = await db
136	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
137	    .from(workers)
138	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
139	    .get();
140	  return record?.turnGeneration ?? null;
141	}
142	
143	/**
144	 * Atomically advance the worker turn fence and (optionally) reset persisted
145	 * worker state into a delivery-safe shape in the SAME mutation, so an
146	 * immediate queued delivery cannot observe stale `working` state after a
147	 * successful cancel. Returns the new generation value.
148	 *
149	 * Callers capture the returned generation for the new delivery; any older
150	 * in-flight completion that captured a smaller value must refuse to persist
151	 * terminal updates (see `isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent`).
152	 */
153	export async function advanceWorkerTurnGeneration(
154	  workerId: string,
155	  reset?: { status?: string; clearCurrentText?: boolean; updatedAt?: Date },
156	): Promise<number> {
157	  await db.update(workers).set({
158	    turnGeneration: sql`${workers.turnGeneration} + 1`,
159	    ...(reset?.status !== undefined ? { status: reset.status } : {}),
160	    ...(reset?.clearCurrentText ? { currentText: "" } : {}),
161	    ...(reset?.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: reset.updatedAt } : {}),
162	  }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
163	  return readWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId);
164	}
165	
166	/**
167	 * True when `capturedGeneration` still matches the worker's persisted fence,
168	 * i.e. no newer interrupt delivery has superseded the captured turn. A missing
169	 * worker row reports `false` so stale callers stop persisting.
170	 */
171	export async function isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(
172	  workerId: string,
173	  capturedGeneration: number,
174	): Promise<boolean> {
175	  const record = await db
176	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
177	    .from(workers)
178	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
179	    .get();
180	  if (!record) {
181	    return false;
182	  }
183	  return record.turnGeneration === capturedGeneration;
184	}
185	
186	export async function runConversationMutation<T>(runId: string, task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
187	  if (activeConversationMutation.getStore() === runId) {
188	    await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
189	    return task();
190	  }
191	  let operation!: Promise<T>;
192	  await withRunWorkspaceMutationAdmission(runId, () => {
193	    operation = runOnChain(conversationMutationChains, runId, async () => {
194	      await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);
195	      return activeConversationMutation.run(runId, task);
196	    });
197	  });
198	  return operation;
199	}
200	
201	export async function waitForConversationMutations(runId: string, timeoutMs = 30_000): Promise<void> {
202	  const operation = conversationMutationChains.get(runId);
203	  if (!operation) return;
204	  let timeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
205	  try {
206	    await Promise.race([
207	      operation,
208	      new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
209	        timeout = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out waiting for conversation mutation ${runId}`)), timeoutMs);
210	      }),
211	    ]);
212	  } finally {
213	    if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
214	  }
215	}
216	
217	export function trackConversationBackgroundTask<T>(
218	  task: Promise<T>,
219	  options: { runId?: string } = {},
220	): Promise<T> {
221	  const runId = options.runId?.trim() || null;
222	  const tracked = task.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
223	  backgroundTasks.add(tracked);
224	  if (runId) {
225	    const runTasks = backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId) ?? new Set<Promise<void>>();
226	    runTasks.add(tracked);
227	    backgroundTasksByRunId.set(runId, runTasks);
228	  }
229	  void tracked.finally(() => {
230	    backgroundTasks.delete(tracked);
231	    if (runId) {
232	      const runTasks = backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId);
233	      runTasks?.delete(tracked);
234	      if (!runTasks || runTasks.size === 0) {
235	        backgroundTasksByRunId.delete(runId);
236	      }
237	      if (completedConversationDeletionRequests.has(runId) && !backgroundTasksByRunId.has(runId)) {
238	        completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
239	        conversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
240	      }
241	    }
242	  });
243	  return task;
244	}
245	
246	export function requestConversationDeletion(runId: string): void {
247	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
248	  conversationDeletionRequests.add(runId);
249	}
250	
251	export function isConversationDeletionRequested(runId: string): boolean {
252	  return conversationDeletionRequests.has(runId);
253	}
254	
255	export function completeConversationDeletion(runId: string): void {
256	  if (!backgroundTasksByRunId.has(runId)) {
257	    conversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
258	    completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
259	    return;
260	  }
261	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.add(runId);
262	}
263	
264	export async function waitForConversationBackgroundTasks(
265	  runId: string,
266	  timeoutMs = 30_000,
267	): Promise<void> {
268	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
269	  while ((backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId)?.size ?? 0) > 0) {
270	    const remaining = deadline - Date.now();
271	    const runTasks = Array.from(backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId) ?? []);
272	    if (remaining <= 0) {
273	      throw new Error(
274	        `Timed out waiting for ${runTasks.length} background task(s) for conversation ${runId}`,
275	      );
276	    }
277	    await Promise.race([
278	      Promise.all(runTasks),
279	      new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(remaining, 25))),
280	    ]);
281	  }
282	}
283	
284	export async function waitForConversationBackgroundTasksForTests(timeoutMs = 1_000): Promise<void> {
285	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
286	  while (backgroundTasks.size > 0) {
287	    const remaining = deadline - Date.now();
288	    if (remaining <= 0) {
289	      throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${backgroundTasks.size} conversation background task(s)`);
290	    }
291	    await Promise.race([
292	      Promise.all(Array.from(backgroundTasks)),
293	      new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(remaining, 25))),
294	    ]);
295	  }
296	}
297	
298	export function __resetWorkerTurnChainsForTests() {
299	  workerTurnChains.clear();
300	  conversationMutationChains.clear();
301	  backgroundTasks.clear();
302	  backgroundTasksByRunId.clear();
303	  conversationDeletionRequests.clear();
304	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.clear();
305	}
```

```text
7	type QueuedConversationMessageRecord = typeof queuedConversationMessages.$inferSelect;

9	export function serializeQueuedConversationMessage(record: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
10	  return {
11	    id: record.id,
12	    runId: record.runId,
13	    targetWorkerId: record.targetWorkerId,
14	    action: record.action as BusyMessageAction,
15	    content: record.content,
16	    status: record.status as QueuedConversationMessageStatus,
17	    lastError: record.lastError,
18	    attachments: normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []),
19	    createdAt: record.createdAt.toISOString(),
20	    updatedAt: record.updatedAt.toISOString(),
21	    deliveredAt: record.deliveredAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
22	  };
```

```text
3	import { StateManager } from "@/lib/state-manager";
4	import type { QueuedConversationMessageRecord } from "./types";

7	type BusyMessageQueueState = {
8	  queuedMessages: QueuedConversationMessageRecord[];
9	  cancellingMessageIds: Set<string>;
10	  // Rows the user has asked to interrupt-and-send ("force send"). Optimistic
11	  // UI only — server queue rows from /api/events remain authoritative.
12	  interruptingMessageIds: Set<string>;
13	  locallyHiddenMessageIds: Set<string>;
14	  serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById: Map<string, number>;
15	  // Rows this client queued whose POST has not returned. The server cannot
16	  // have them yet, so "absent from the server list" must not be read as
17	  // "cancelled" for these — otherwise the row the user just queued blinks out
18	  // on the next event frame and back in when the POST lands.
19	  pendingQueuedMessageIds: Set<string>;
20	};
21	
22	type QueueSnapshotOptions = {
23	  runId?: string | null;
24	  notify?: boolean;
25	} | boolean;
26	
27	const initialBusyMessageQueueState: BusyMessageQueueState = {
28	  queuedMessages: [],
29	  cancellingMessageIds: new Set(),
30	  interruptingMessageIds: new Set(),
31	  locallyHiddenMessageIds: new Set(),
32	  serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById: new Map(),
33	  pendingQueuedMessageIds: new Set(),
34	};
35	
36	function isActiveQueuedMessage(message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
37	  return message.status === "pending" || message.status === "delivering";
38	}
39	
40	function timestampMs(value: string | null | undefined) {
41	  if (!value) {
42	    return 0;
43	  }
44	  const parsed = new Date(value).getTime();
45	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
46	}
47	
48	function isStaleServerAbsentActiveMessage(
49	  message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord,
50	  serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById: Map<string, number>,
51	) {
52	  if (!isActiveQueuedMessage(message)) {
53	    return false;
54	  }
55	  const absentUpdatedAt = serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.get(message.id);
56	  return absentUpdatedAt !== undefined && timestampMs(message.updatedAt) <= absentUpdatedAt;
57	}
58	
59	function attachmentsEqual(
60	  left: QueuedConversationMessageRecord["attachments"],
61	  right: QueuedConversationMessageRecord["attachments"],
62	) {
63	  const leftAttachments = left ?? [];
64	  const rightAttachments = right ?? [];
65	  if (leftAttachments.length !== rightAttachments.length) {
66	    return false;
67	  }
68	  return leftAttachments.every((attachment, index) => JSON.stringify(attachment) === JSON.stringify(rightAttachments[index]));
69	}
70	
71	function queuedMessageRecordsEqual(left: QueuedConversationMessageRecord, right: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
72	  return left.id === right.id
73	    && left.runId === right.runId
74	    && left.targetWorkerId === right.targetWorkerId
75	    && left.action === right.action
76	    && left.content === right.content
77	    && left.status === right.status
78	    && left.lastError === right.lastError
79	    && left.createdAt === right.createdAt
80	    && left.updatedAt === right.updatedAt
81	    && left.deliveredAt === right.deliveredAt
82	    && attachmentsEqual(left.attachments, right.attachments);
83	}
84	
85	function queuedMessageArraysEqual(left: QueuedConversationMessageRecord[], right: QueuedConversationMessageRecord[]) {
86	  return left.length === right.length
87	    && left.every((message, index) => queuedMessageRecordsEqual(message, right[index]!));
88	}
89	
90	function setsEqual<T>(left: Set<T>, right: Set<T>) {
91	  if (left === right) {
92	    return true;
93	  }
94	  if (left.size !== right.size) {
95	    return false;
96	  }
97	  for (const value of left) {
98	    if (!right.has(value)) {
99	      return false;
100	    }
101	  }
102	  return true;
103	}
104	
105	function mapsEqual<TKey, TValue>(left: Map<TKey, TValue>, right: Map<TKey, TValue>) {
106	  if (left === right) {
107	    return true;
108	  }
109	  if (left.size !== right.size) {
110	    return false;
111	  }
112	  for (const [key, value] of left) {
113	    if (!Object.is(right.get(key), value)) {
114	      return false;
115	    }
116	  }
117	  return true;
118	}
119	
120	export class BusyMessageQueueManager extends StateManager<BusyMessageQueueState> {
121	  constructor() {
122	    super(initialBusyMessageQueueState);
123	  }
124	
125	  setQueuedMessages(messages: QueuedConversationMessageRecord[], options: QueueSnapshotOptions = {}) {
126	    const hasRunScope = typeof options !== "boolean" && "runId" in options;
127	    const scopedRunId = hasRunScope ? options.runId?.trim() || null : null;
128	    const notify = typeof options === "boolean" ? options : options.notify ?? true;
129	
130	    // A scoped event snapshot with no run id is not authoritative for any
131	    // queue. Legacy boolean callers retain the old whole-state behavior.
132	    if (hasRunScope && !scopedRunId) {
133	      return;
134	    }
135	
136	    this.update((current) => {
137	      const scopedIncomingMessages = scopedRunId
138	        ? messages.filter((message) => message.runId === scopedRunId)
139	        : messages;
140	      const scopedCurrentMessages = scopedRunId
141	        ? current.queuedMessages.filter((message) => message.runId === scopedRunId)
142	        : current.queuedMessages;
143	      const incomingMessages = scopedIncomingMessages.filter(
144	        (message) => !isStaleServerAbsentActiveMessage(message, current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById),
145	      );
146	      const incomingIds = new Set(incomingMessages.map((message) => message.id));
147	      // A row whose POST is still in flight is not "gone from the server", it
148	      // has not reached the server yet. Leave it out of the absence
149	      // bookkeeping entirely so it is neither dropped now nor rejected as
150	      // stale when the real row arrives.
151	      const newlyAbsentActiveIds = scopedCurrentMessages
152	        .filter((message) => (
153	          isActiveQueuedMessage(message)
154	          && !incomingIds.has(message.id)
155	          && !current.pendingQueuedMessageIds.has(message.id)
156	        ))
157	        .map((message) => [message.id, timestampMs(message.updatedAt)] as const);
158	      const retainedPendingMessages = current.queuedMessages.filter((message) => (
159	        current.pendingQueuedMessageIds.has(message.id) && !incomingIds.has(message.id)
160	      ));
161	      // Server rows are authoritative: clear optimistic interrupt/cancel flags
162	      // for any row that has left a pending/delivering state.
163	      const settledIds = new Set(
164	        [
165	          ...incomingMessages
166	            .filter((message) => !isActiveQueuedMessage(message))
167	            .map((message) => message.id),
168	          ...newlyAbsentActiveIds.map(([id]) => id),
169	        ],
170	      );
171	      const clearSettled = (ids: Set<string>) => {
172	        if (settledIds.size === 0) {
173	          return ids;
174	        }
175	        let changed = false;
176	        const next = new Set(ids);
177	        for (const id of settledIds) {
178	          if (next.delete(id)) {
179	            changed = true;
180	          }
181	        }
182	        return changed ? next : ids;
183	      };
184	      const serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById = new Map(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById);

187	      }
188	      for (const message of incomingMessages) {
189	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.delete(message.id);
190	      }
191	      const queuedMessages = [
192	        ...(scopedRunId
193	          ? current.queuedMessages.filter((message) => message.runId !== scopedRunId)
194	          : []),
195	        ...incomingMessages.filter((message) => !current.locallyHiddenMessageIds.has(message.id)),
196	        ...retainedPendingMessages,
197	      ].sort(compareOldestByCreatedAtThenId);
198	      const cancellingMessageIds = clearSettled(current.cancellingMessageIds);
199	      const interruptingMessageIds = clearSettled(current.interruptingMessageIds);
200	      if (
201	        queuedMessageArraysEqual(current.queuedMessages, queuedMessages)
202	        && setsEqual(current.cancellingMessageIds, cancellingMessageIds)
203	        && setsEqual(current.interruptingMessageIds, interruptingMessageIds)
204	        && mapsEqual(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById, serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById)
205	      ) {
206	        return current;
207	      }
208	      return {
209	        ...current,
210	        queuedMessages,
211	        cancellingMessageIds,
212	        interruptingMessageIds,
213	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
214	      };
215	    }, notify);
216	  }
217	
218	  /**
219	   * The rows the drawer should actually render.
220	   *
221	   * A message id must appear exactly once across the whole conversation
222	   * surface, but the transcript and this drawer are separate lists, so neither
223	   * one's dedup can see the other's copy. The composer predicts busy-ness from
224	   * event-stream state, so it can render a message as a transcript bubble that
225	   * the server then decides to queue; the queue row reaches the client on the
226	   * event stream before the POST returns, and the text is on screen twice
227	   * until it does. Suppress the row for as long as the send owns a transcript
228	   * bubble — when the POST settles it decides which surface keeps the message.
229	   *
230	   * `inFlightSentMessageIds` is passed in rather than mirrored into this
231	   * manager's state: `SentConversationMessagesManager` already owns that fact,
232	   * and a second copy could disagree with it.
233	   */
234	  getVisibleQueuedMessagesForRun(
235	    runId: string | null | undefined,
236	    inFlightSentMessageIds: ReadonlySet<string>,
237	  ) {
238	    return this.getQueuedMessagesForRun(runId)
239	      .filter((message) => !inFlightSentMessageIds.has(message.id));
240	  }
241	
242	  getQueuedMessagesForRun(runId: string | null | undefined) {
243	    const normalizedRunId = runId?.trim();
244	    if (!normalizedRunId) {
245	      return [];
246	    }
247	    return this.getSnapshot().queuedMessages.filter((message) => message.runId === normalizedRunId);
248	  }
249	
250	  upsertQueuedMessage(message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
251	    this.update((current) => {
252	      const serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById = new Map(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById);
253	      if (isStaleServerAbsentActiveMessage(message, serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById)) {
254	        return current;
255	      }
256	
257	      const cancellingMessageIds = new Set(current.cancellingMessageIds);
258	      const interruptingMessageIds = new Set(current.interruptingMessageIds);
259	      if (!isActiveQueuedMessage(message)) {
260	        cancellingMessageIds.delete(message.id);
261	        interruptingMessageIds.delete(message.id);
262	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.set(message.id, timestampMs(message.updatedAt));
263	        return {
264	          ...current,
265	          queuedMessages: current.queuedMessages.filter((entry) => entry.id !== message.id),
266	          cancellingMessageIds,
267	          interruptingMessageIds,
268	          serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
269	        };
270	      }
271	
272	      serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.delete(message.id);
273	      const existingIndex = current.queuedMessages.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === message.id);
274	      if (existingIndex === -1) {
275	        return {
276	          ...current,
277	          queuedMessages: [...current.queuedMessages, message].sort(compareOldestByCreatedAtThenId),
278	          serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
279	        };
280	      }
281	
282	      const next = [...current.queuedMessages];
283	      next[existingIndex] = message;
284	      return {
285	        ...current,
286	        queuedMessages: next,
287	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
288	      };
289	    });
290	  }
291	
292	  /**
293	   * Show a row in the queue from the moment the user hits send, rather than
294	   * after the POST returns. A message sent with `busyAction: "queue"` is
295	   * always queued server-side, so the prediction cannot be wrong — and
296	   * rendering it as a sent bubble in the meantime made it appear in the
297	   * transcript for the length of the round trip before jumping to the queue.
298	   */
299	  beginQueueSend(message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
300	    this.patch((current) => {
301	      const pendingQueuedMessageIds = new Set(current.pendingQueuedMessageIds);
302	      pendingQueuedMessageIds.add(message.id);
303	      const serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById = new Map(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById);
304	      serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.delete(message.id);
305	      return {
306	        queuedMessages: [...current.queuedMessages, message].sort(compareOldestByCreatedAtThenId),
307	        pendingQueuedMessageIds,
308	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
309	      };
310	    });
311	  }
312	
313	  /**
314	   * The POST returned. The server row normally carries the id the optimistic
315	   * row already used, so this is an in-place swap; a server that minted its
316	   * own id instead drops the optimistic row and takes the returned one.
317	   */
318	  settleQueueSend(pendingMessageId: string, message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord | null | undefined) {
319	    this.patch((current) => {
320	      const pendingQueuedMessageIds = new Set(current.pendingQueuedMessageIds);
321	      pendingQueuedMessageIds.delete(pendingMessageId);
322	      const queuedMessages = message && message.id !== pendingMessageId
323	        ? current.queuedMessages.filter((entry) => entry.id !== pendingMessageId)
324	        : current.queuedMessages;
325	      return { pendingQueuedMessageIds, queuedMessages };
326	    });
327	    if (message) {
328	      this.upsertQueuedMessage(message);
329	    }
330	  }
331	
332	  failQueueSend(pendingMessageId: string) {
333	    this.patch((current) => {
334	      const pendingQueuedMessageIds = new Set(current.pendingQueuedMessageIds);
335	      pendingQueuedMessageIds.delete(pendingMessageId);
336	      return {
337	        pendingQueuedMessageIds,
338	        queuedMessages: current.queuedMessages.filter((entry) => entry.id !== pendingMessageId),
339	      };
340	    });
341	  }
342	
343	  markCancelling(messageId: string) {
344	    this.setKey("cancellingMessageIds", (current) => new Set([...current, messageId]));
345	  }
346	
347	  unmarkCancelling(messageId: string) {
348	    this.setKey("cancellingMessageIds", (current) => {
349	      const next = new Set(current);
350	      next.delete(messageId);
351	      return next;
352	    });
353	  }
354	
355	  markInterrupting(messageId: string) {
356	    this.setKey("interruptingMessageIds", (current) => new Set([...current, messageId]));
357	  }
358	
359	  unmarkInterrupting(messageId: string) {
360	    this.setKey("interruptingMessageIds", (current) => {
361	      const next = new Set(current);
362	      next.delete(messageId);
363	      return next;
364	    });
365	  }
366	
367	  hideQueuedMessage(messageId: string) {
368	    this.patch((current) => {
369	      const cancellingMessageIds = new Set(current.cancellingMessageIds);
370	      const interruptingMessageIds = new Set(current.interruptingMessageIds);
371	      const locallyHiddenMessageIds = new Set(current.locallyHiddenMessageIds);
372	      cancellingMessageIds.delete(messageId);
373	      interruptingMessageIds.delete(messageId);
374	      locallyHiddenMessageIds.add(messageId);
375	      const serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById = new Map(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById);
376	      const existing = current.queuedMessages.find((message) => message.id === messageId);
377	      serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.set(messageId, timestampMs(existing?.updatedAt));
378	      return {
379	        queuedMessages: current.queuedMessages.filter((message) => message.id !== messageId),
380	        cancellingMessageIds,
381	        interruptingMessageIds,
382	        locallyHiddenMessageIds,
383	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
384	      };
385	    });
386	  }
387	
388	  restoreQueuedMessage(message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
389	    this.patch((current) => {
390	      const cancellingMessageIds = new Set(current.cancellingMessageIds);
391	      const interruptingMessageIds = new Set(current.interruptingMessageIds);
392	      const locallyHiddenMessageIds = new Set(current.locallyHiddenMessageIds);
393	      const serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById = new Map(current.serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById);
394	      cancellingMessageIds.delete(message.id);
395	      interruptingMessageIds.delete(message.id);
396	      locallyHiddenMessageIds.delete(message.id);
397	      serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById.delete(message.id);
398	      const existingIndex = current.queuedMessages.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === message.id);
399	      const queuedMessages = existingIndex === -1
400	        ? [...current.queuedMessages, message].sort(compareOldestByCreatedAtThenId)
401	        : current.queuedMessages.map((entry) => entry.id === message.id ? message : entry);
402	      return {
403	        queuedMessages,
404	        cancellingMessageIds,
405	        interruptingMessageIds,
406	        locallyHiddenMessageIds,
407	        serverAbsentMessageUpdatedAtById,
408	      };
409	    });
410	  }
411	}
```

```text
export const queuedConversationMessages = sqliteTable('queued_conversation_messages', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  runId: text('run_id').references(() => runs.id).notNull(),
  targetWorkerId: text('target_worker_id').references(() => workers.id),
  action: text('action').notNull(),
  content: text('content').notNull(),
  attachmentsJson: text('attachments_json'),
  status: text('status').notNull(),
  lastError: text('last_error'),
  createdAt: integer('created_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  updatedAt: integer('updated_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  deliveredAt: integer('delivered_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
});

export const recoveryIncidents = sqliteTable('recovery_incidents', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  runId: text('run_id').references(() => runs.id).notNull(),
  workerId: text('worker_id').references(() => workers.id),
  queuedMessageId: text('queued_message_id').references(() => queuedConversationMessages.id),
  kind: text('kind').notNull(),
  status: text('status').notNull(),
  autoAttemptCount: integer('auto_attempt_count').notNull().default(0),
  lastError: text('last_error'),
  details: text('details'),
  detectedAt: integer('detected_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  updatedAt: integer('updated_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  resolvedAt: integer('resolved_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
});

export const supervisorScheduledWakes = sqliteTable('supervisor_scheduled_wakes', {
  runId: text('run_id').primaryKey().references(() => runs.id),
  wakeAt: integer('wake_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  reason: text('reason').notNull(),
  source: text('source'),
  incidentId: text('incident_id').references(() => recoveryIncidents.id),
  details: text('details'),
  createdAt: integer('created_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
  updatedAt: integer('updated_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
});
```

```text
305	function isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(status: string) {
306	  const normalized = normalizedStatus(status);
307	  return Boolean(normalized) && !["starting", "working", "stuck", "error", "cancelled"].includes(normalized);
308	}
309	
310	async function pendingWorkerQueueCount(runId: string, workerId: string) {
311	  const records = await db.select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id })
312	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
313	    .where(and(
314	      eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId),
315	      eq(queuedConversationMessages.targetWorkerId, workerId),
316	      eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "pending"),
317	    ));
318	  return records.length;
319	}
320	
321	async function recordQueueDrainDecision(args: {
322	  runId: string;
323	  workerId: string;
324	  source: string;
325	  workerStatus: string;
326	  pendingCount: number;
327	  decision: "drain" | "skip";
328	  reason: string;
329	}) {
330	  emitNamedEvent({
331	    kind: "queue.drain_decision",
332	    runId: args.runId,
333	    workerId: args.workerId,
334	    source: args.source,
335	    workerStatus: args.workerStatus,
336	    pendingCount: args.pendingCount,
337	    decision: args.decision,
338	    reason: args.reason,
339	  });
340	  await recordExecutionEvent({
341	    runId: args.runId,
342	    workerId: args.workerId,
343	    eventType: "queue_drain_decision",
344	    details: {
345	      summary: args.decision === "drain"
346	        ? `Draining ${args.pendingCount} queued message(s) for ${args.workerId}.`
347	        : `Skipped queue drain for ${args.workerId}: ${args.reason}.`,
348	      source: args.source,
349	      workerStatus: args.workerStatus,
350	      pendingCount: args.pendingCount,
351	      decision: args.decision,
352	      reason: args.reason,
353	    },
354	  });
355	}
356	
357	async function drainQueuedWorkerMessagesWithObservation(args: {
358	  runId: string;
359	  workerId: string;
360	  workerStatus: string;
361	  source: string;
362	  snapshot?: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord> | null;
363	}) {
364	  const pendingCount = await pendingWorkerQueueCount(args.runId, args.workerId);
365	  if (pendingCount === 0) {
366	    return 0;
367	  }
368	
369	  const snapshot = args.snapshot ?? null;
370	  const hasPendingElicitation = hasPendingElicitationSignal(snapshot);
371	  const drainable = hasPendingElicitation || isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(args.workerStatus);
372	  await recordQueueDrainDecision({
373	    ...args,
374	    pendingCount,
375	    decision: drainable ? "drain" : "skip",
376	    reason: hasPendingElicitation
377	      ? "pending_elicitation"
378	      : drainable
379	        ? "worker_drainable"
380	        : "worker_not_drainable",
381	  });
382	  if (!drainable) {
383	    return 0;
384	  }
385	
386	  // Deliver on a background task rather than inline. Sync passes are
387	  // serialized through `liveSyncQueue`, and the drain runs a full worker turn:
388	  // awaiting it here parked the entire sync chain for the length of the turn.
389	  // When the agent raised an elicitation mid-turn that became a deadlock — the
390	  // turn cannot finish until the question is answered, and the question is
391	  // only surfaced by the snapshot writes in the very sync pass that is stuck
392	  // waiting for the turn. Claiming is atomic (`status = 'pending'` guard), and
393	  // an in-flight delivery leaves `pendingCount` at 0, so overlapping passes
394	  // return above instead of double-delivering.
395	  const delivery = trackConversationBackgroundTask((async () => {
396	    const deliveredCount = await drainQueuedWorkerMessages({
397	      runId: args.runId,
398	      workerId: args.workerId,
399	      snapshot,
400	    });
401	    emitNamedEvent({
402	      kind: "queue.drain_finished",
403	      runId: args.runId,
404	      workerId: args.workerId,
405	      source: args.source,
406	      pendingCount,
407	      deliveredCount,
408	    });
409	    await recordExecutionEvent({
410	      runId: args.runId,
411	      workerId: args.workerId,
412	      eventType: "queue_drain_finished",
413	      details: {
414	        summary: `Queue drain finished for ${args.workerId}: delivered ${deliveredCount} of ${pendingCount}.`,
415	        source: args.source,
416	        pendingCount,
417	        deliveredCount,
418	      },
419	    });
420	    return deliveredCount;
421	  })(), { runId: args.runId });
422	  delivery.catch((error) => {
423	    console.error(`Queued message drain failed for ${args.workerId}:`, error);
424	  });
```

```text
63	    latest = await read();
64	  }
65	
66	  return latest;
67	}
```

```text
CREATE TABLE runs (
  id text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,

  status text NOT NULL,

CREATE TRIGGER runs_activity_seed

WHEN NEW.last_activity_at IS NULL
BEGIN

END;

CREATE INDEX runs_active_handoff_idx ON runs(active_handoff_id);
```

```text
CREATE TABLE messages (
  id text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  run_id text NOT NULL,
  role text NOT NULL,
  content text NOT NULL,
  worker_id text,
  created_at integer NOT NULL, kind text, superseded_at integer, edited_from_message_id text, attachments_json text,
  FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES runs(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action,

CREATE INDEX messages_run_created_id_idx ON messages(run_id, created_at, id);
CREATE TRIGGER runs_activity_on_user_message
AFTER INSERT ON messages
WHEN lower(NEW.role) = 'user'
  AND lower(COALESCE(NEW.kind, '')) NOT IN ('internal', 'intervention')
BEGIN
  UPDATE runs
  SET last_activity_at = NEW.created_at
  WHERE id = NEW.run_id
    AND COALESCE(last_activity_at, 0) < NEW.created_at;
END;
```

```text
  FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES runs(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action,
  FOREIGN KEY (worker_id) REFERENCES workers(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action,
  FOREIGN KEY (plan_item_id) REFERENCES plan_items(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action
);
CREATE INDEX execution_events_run_created_idx ON execution_events(run_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX execution_events_run_created_id_desc_idx ON execution_events(run_id, created_at DESC, id DESC);
```

## Claude subagent agent-a079ac6be22c9ace7: Claude · 2026-08-21T22:22:43.415Z

````text
Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile the comprehensive lifecycle mapping:

## Full Lifecycle of Queued Messages

### 1. Storage Locations (Files)

The queued messages system uses these files:

- **Core logic**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts`
- **Interrupt handler**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-message-interrupt.ts`
- **Message records/serialization**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-message-records.ts`
- **Sync reconciliation**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts`
- **Turn gating/locking**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/worker-turn-gate.ts`
- **Database schema**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/db/schema.ts`
- **UI state management**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/BusyMessageQueueManager.ts`
- **Supervisor integration**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/supervisor/index.ts`

### 2. Status Lifecycle and Values

Messages cycle through these exact states (from `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-message-records.ts:5`):

```
type QueuedConversationMessageStatus = "pending" | "delivering" | "delivered" | "cancelled" | "failed";
```

**State flow:**
- `pending` → `delivering` → `delivered` (success path)
- `pending` → `delivering` → `pending` (busy/deferred, retry)
- `pending` → `delivering` → `failed` (non-recoverable error)
- `pending` → `cancelled` (user cancel)
- Any state can be orphaned at `delivering` if process crashes

### 3. Queue Drain Triggers and Paths

#### **Path 1: Live Worker Sync (Conversation Mode)**

**Trigger:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:854-860`

Worker status changes detected by bridge are synced, then drain is triggered:
```typescript
await drainQueuedWorkerMessagesWithObservation({
  runId: run.id,
  workerId: worker.id,
  workerStatus: nextWorkerStatus,
  source: "live_worker_sync",
  snapshot: agent,
});
```

**Decision gate:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:305-308`

```typescript
function isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(status: string) {
  const normalized = normalizedStatus(status);
  return Boolean(normalized) && !["starting", "working", "stuck", "error", "cancelled"].includes(normalized);
}
```

Drain proceeds if worker status is **NOT** one of: `starting`, `working`, `stuck`, `error`, `cancelled`.

#### **Path 2: Live Planning Sync**

**Trigger:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:819-824`

For planning mode runs, drain is called with `source: "live_planning_sync"`.

#### **Path 3: Persisted State Unchanged (Direct/Offline)**

**Trigger:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:912-919`

When a run's status hasn't changed but worker exists:
```typescript
if (nextRunState === run.status) {
  await drainQueuedWorkerMessagesWithObservation({
    runId: run.id,
    workerId: worker.id,
    workerStatus: worker.status,
    source: "persisted_state_unchanged",
  });
```

#### **Path 4: Implementation/Supervisor Mode**

**Trigger:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/supervisor/index.ts:34` and called around line ~710 in supervisor run loop

Supervisor calls directly without sync wrapper:
```typescript
await drainQueuedImplementationMessages(this.runId);
```

### 4. Drain Execution: The Critical Function

**Entry point (wraps in conversation mutex):** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1475-1477`

```typescript
export function drainQueuedWorkerMessages(args: Parameters<typeof drainQueuedWorkerMessagesUnlocked>[0]) {
  return runConversationMutation(args.runId, () => drainQueuedWorkerMessagesUnlocked(args));
}
```

**Core drain logic:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1218-1477`

### 5. Key Guards That Prevent Message Delivery Forever

#### **Guard 1: Worker Missing (Early Return)**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1228-1231`

```typescript
const worker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, workerId)).get();
if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId) {
  return 0;
}
```

If worker is not found or doesn't belong to run, drain exits immediately. **No message is touched.**

#### **Guard 2: Run Missing (Early Return)**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1233-1236`

```typescript
const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
if (!run) {
  return 0;
}
```

If run is not found, drain exits. **Messages remain in their current state.**

#### **Guard 3: Status CAS (Compare-and-Swap) at Claim**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1261-1271`

```typescript
const claimed = await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
  status: "delivering",
  updatedAt: startedAt,
  lastError: null,
}).where(and(
  eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id),
  eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "pending"),  // <-- ATOMIC CONDITION
)).returning({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id });
if (claimed.length === 0) {
  continue;  // Skips if already claimed or not pending
}
```

Only messages with status exactly `"pending"` can be claimed. Multiple concurrent drains safely dedup.

#### **Guard 4: Pending Elicitation Self-Deadlock Prevention**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1279-1282`

```typescript
// A pending elicitation belongs to the turn that is already running. Do
// not queue its answer behind that same turn: the turn cannot finish until
// the answer arrives, so acquiring the per-worker turn gate here creates a
// self-deadlock and leaves the queue row in `delivering` forever.
if (selectPendingWorkerElicitation(snapshot ?? null)) {
  // ... answer without entering turn gate ...
}
```

If a pending elicitation exists, the answer is delivered **outside the worker turn lock** to avoid deadlock.

#### **Guard 5: Turn Generation Fence (Interrupt Supersede)**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-message-interrupt.ts:397-406`

```typescript
const isStillCurrent = async (appended = false) => {
  if (await queuedRowStatus(record.id) !== "delivering") {
    return false;
  }
  if (await isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(worker.id, generation)) {
    return true;
  }
  await releaseSupersededRow(appended);  // Goes back to pending if not appended yet
  return false;
};
```

Interrupt deliveries check turn generation at multiple points. If a newer interrupt arrives, older delivery releases the row.

#### **Guard 6: Handoff Fence**
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1227, 1029-1030`

```typescript
await assertRunNotHandoffFenced(runId);  // Throws if run is handoff-fenced
```

If run is locked for handoff, drain throws and exits without touching messages.

### 6. Error Handling During Drain

**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:1418-1465`

- **Quota exhaustion**: Message reverts to `pending` for retry
- **Agent busy error** (`isAgentBusyError`): Message reverts to `pending`, user input removed
- **Agent not found** (`isAgentNotFoundError`): Message stays `delivering` indefinitely UNLESS recovery reconciler is triggered
- **Empty output error** (`isEmptyQueuedWorkerOutputError`): Message marked `failed`, worker marked `error`
- **Other errors**: Message marked `failed`, drain breaks (stops processing further messages in this batch)

### 7. Orphaned "Delivering" Messages Recovery

**Trigger:** Process startup (boot recovery)
**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/runner/run.ts` (calls during initialization)

**Function:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts:472-498`

```typescript
export async function reclaimOrphanedDeliveringMessages() {
  const orphaned = await db
    .select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id, runId: queuedConversationMessages.runId })
    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "delivering"));  // <-- Find orphans
  if (orphaned.length === 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  const now = new Date();
  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
    status: "pending",  // <-- Reclaim as pending
    lastError: null,
    updatedAt: now,
    deliveredAt: null,
  }).where(inArray(queuedConversationMessages.id, orphaned.map((record) => record.id)));
  // ... emit events ...
}
```

Any message left in `delivering` state survives a restart and gets reset to `pending` at boot.

### 8. Frontend State: "Queued" Badge Driver

**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/BusyMessageQueueManager.ts:36-38`

```typescript
function isActiveQueuedMessage(message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) {
  return message.status === "pending" || message.status === "delivering";
}
```

UI shows "queued" badge for messages with status `pending` OR `delivering`. These are the only two states visible to the user as "active queue."

### 9. Potential Forever-Queued Scenarios

**Scenario A: Agent Not Found During Drain**
- Message moves to `delivering`
- Drain calls `askAgent`, gets "agent not found" error
- Error handler at line 1447-1457 triggers recovery but keeps message at `delivering`
- If recovery reconciler cannot restore agent, message hangs

**Scenario B: Handoff Fence Blocks Everything**
- Drain exits early at line 1227
- All pending messages remain `pending` but drain is never retried
- Sync passes refuse to drain because `assertRunNotHandoffFenced` fails

**Scenario C: Worker Missing at Drain Time**
- Drain exits at line 1228-1231
- Messages never touched; remain in whatever state they were in
- No subsequent drain attempt until sync is called again

**Scenario D: Claimed Message Never Reaches Turn Gate**
- Message claimed to `delivering` at line 1261
- Something between lines 1272-1325 throws but it's NOT caught (e.g., process crash)
- Message stays `delivering` until boot recovery reclaims it

**Scenario E: Supervisor Intervention Lock Stuck**
- `drainQueuedImplementationMessages` at line 1071 calls `runWorkerTurn(worker.id, async () => { ... })`
- If that turn gate itself hangs (previous turn still executing), the drain waits indefinitely
- Message remains `delivering` for the duration

### 10. Sync Decision Tree

**Location:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:371`

Drain decision logic:
```typescript
const hasPendingElicitation = hasPendingElicitationSignal(snapshot);
const drainable = hasPendingElicitation || isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(args.workerStatus);
```

- If `hasPendingElicitation = true`: Drain ALWAYS happens
- Else if `isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus = true`: Drain happens
- Else: Drain is skipped, reason logged as "worker_not_drainable"

**Non-drainable statuses:** `starting`, `working`, `stuck`, `error`, `cancelled` (plus empty/unrecognized strings normalize to `""` which is falsy).

### Summary: When Queued Messages Stay Stuck

Messages can remain queued indefinitely in these cases:

1. **Worker deleted/missing** from database before drain
2. **Run deleted/missing** from database before drain
3. **Handoff fence active** prevents all drain attempts
4. **Agent not found** during interrupt delivery and recovery fails
5. **Message stuck `delivering`** and process never restarts (boot recovery doesn't happen)
6. **Worker turn gate deadlock** (elicitation mid-turn without early-check optimization)
7. **Process crash** after CAS claim but before turn entry

The most insidious is #4+#5: if agent goes missing during an interrupt delivery, the message is marked `delivering`, recovery is triggered but may not succeed, and without a process restart, that message is invisible (not shown in queue drawer) but never delivered.
````
